## Accessibility

- **AccessibilityActionCategory** `struct AccessibilityActionCategory` - Designates an accessibility action category that is provided and named   by the system. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/accessibilityactioncategory))
- **AccessibilityActionKind** `struct AccessibilityActionKind` - The structure that defines the kinds of available accessibility actions. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/accessibilityactionkind))
- **AccessibilityAdjustmentDirection** `enum AccessibilityAdjustmentDirection` - A directional indicator you use when making an accessibility adjustment. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/accessibilityadjustmentdirection))
- **AccessibilityAttachmentModifier** `struct AccessibilityAttachmentModifier` - A view modifier that adds accessibility properties to the view ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/accessibilityattachmentmodifier))
- **AccessibilityChildBehavior** `struct AccessibilityChildBehavior` - Defines the behavior for the child elements of the new parent element. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/accessibilitychildbehavior))
- **AccessibilityCustomContentKey** `struct AccessibilityCustomContentKey` - Key used to specify the identifier and label associated with   an entry of additional accessibility information. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/accessibilitycustomcontentkey))
- **AccessibilityDirectTouchOptions** `struct AccessibilityDirectTouchOptions` - An option set that defines the functionality of a view’s direct touch area. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/accessibilitydirecttouchoptions))
- **AccessibilityFocusState** `struct AccessibilityFocusState` - A property wrapper type that can read and write a value that SwiftUI updates   as the focus of any active accessibility technology, such as VoiceOver,   changes. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/accessibilityfocusstate))
- **AccessibilityHeadingLevel** `enum AccessibilityHeadingLevel` - The hierarchy of a heading in relation to other headings. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/accessibilityheadinglevel))
- **AccessibilityLabeledPairRole** `enum AccessibilityLabeledPairRole` - The role of an accessibility element in a label / content pair. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/accessibilitylabeledpairrole))
- **AccessibilityQuickActionStyle** `protocol AccessibilityQuickActionStyle` - A type that describes the presentation style of an   accessibility quick action. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/accessibilityquickactionstyle))
- **AccessibilityRotorContent** `protocol AccessibilityRotorContent` - Content within an accessibility rotor. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/accessibilityrotorcontent))
- **AccessibilityRotorContentBuilder** `struct AccessibilityRotorContentBuilder` - Result builder you use to generate rotor entry content. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/accessibilityrotorcontentbuilder))
- **AccessibilityRotorEntry** `struct AccessibilityRotorEntry` - A struct representing an entry in an Accessibility Rotor. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/accessibilityrotorentry))
- **AccessibilitySystemRotor** `struct AccessibilitySystemRotor` - Designates a Rotor that replaces one of the automatic, system-provided   Rotors with a developer-provided Rotor. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/accessibilitysystemrotor))
- **AccessibilityTechnologies** `struct AccessibilityTechnologies` - Accessibility technologies available to the system. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/accessibilitytechnologies))
- **AccessibilityTextContentType** `struct AccessibilityTextContentType` - Textual context that assistive technologies can use to improve the   presentation of spoken text. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/accessibilitytextcontenttype))
- **AccessibilityTraits** `struct AccessibilityTraits` - A set of accessibility traits that describe how an element behaves. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/accessibilitytraits))
- **AccessibilityZoomGestureAction** `struct AccessibilityZoomGestureAction` - Position and direction information of a zoom gesture that someone performs   with an assistive technology like VoiceOver. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/accessibilityzoomgestureaction))
- **ContentSizeCategory.accessibilityLarge** `case accessibilityLarge` ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/contentsizecategory/accessibilitylarge))

([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/accessibilityaction(_:_:)))
- **accessibilityAction(_:intent:)** `func accessibilityAction<I>(AccessibilityActionKind, intent: I) -> ModifiedContent<Self, AccessibilityAttachmentModifier>` - Adds an accessibility action representing  actionKind  to the view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/accessibilityaction(_:intent:)))

Actions allow assistive technologies, such as the VoiceOver, to interact   with the view by invoking the action. When the action is performed, the   intent  will be invoked. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/accessibilityaction(intent:label:)))

- **accessibilityAction(named:intent:)** `func accessibilityAction(named:intent:)` - Adds an accessibility action labeled  name  to the view. - **accessibilityActions(_:)** `func accessibilityActions<Content>(() -> Content) -> some View` - Adds multiple accessibility actions to the view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/accessibilityactions(_:)))
- **accessibilityActions(category:_:)** `func accessibilityActions<Content>(category: AccessibilityActionCategory, () -> Content) -> some View` - Adds multiple accessibility actions to the view with a specific   category. Actions allow assistive technologies, such as VoiceOver,   to interact with the view by invoking the action and are grouped by   their category. When multiple action modifiers with an equal category   are applied to the view, the actions are combined together. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/accessibilityactions(category:_:)))

- **accessibilityDirectTouch(_:options:)** `func accessibilityDirectTouch(Bool, options: AccessibilityDirectTouchOptions) -> ModifiedContent<Self, AccessibilityAttachmentModifier>` - Explicitly set whether this accessibility element is a direct touch   area. Direct touch areas passthrough touch events to the app rather   than being handled through an assistive technology, such as VoiceOver.   The modifier accepts an optional  AccessibilityDirectTouchOptions   option set to customize the functionality of the direct touch area. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/accessibilitydirecttouch(_:options:)))

## Animations

- **Animation** `struct Animation` - The way a view changes over time to create a smooth visual transition from   one state to another. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/animation))
- **AnimationCompletionCriteria** `struct AnimationCompletionCriteria` - The criteria that determines when an animation is considered finished. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/animationcompletioncriteria))
- **AnimationContext** `struct AnimationContext` - Contextual values that a custom animation can use to manage state and   access a view’s environment. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/animationcontext))
- **AnimationState** `struct AnimationState` - A container that stores the state for a custom animation. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/animationstate))
- **AnimationStateKey** `protocol AnimationStateKey` - A key for accessing animation state values. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/animationstatekey))
- **CustomAnimation** `protocol CustomAnimation` - A type that defines how an animatable value changes over time. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/customanimation))
- **animation(_:)** `func animation(Animation?) -> Binding<Value>` - Specifies an animation to perform when the binding value changes. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/binding/animation(_:)))
- **animation(_:)** `func animation(_:)` - Applies the given animation to this view when this view changes. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/animation(_:)))
- **animation(_:)** `func animation(Animation?) -> some View` - Applies the given animation to all animatable values within this view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/animation(_:)-1hc0p))
- **animation(_:body:)** `func animation<V>(Animation?, body: (PlaceholderContentView<Self>) -> V) -> some View` - Applies the given animation to all animatable values within the  body   closure. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/animation(_:body:)))
- **animation(_:value:)** `func animation<V>(Animation?, value: V) -> some View` - Applies the given animation to this view when the specified value   changes. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/animation(_:value:)))
- **bouncy** `static var bouncy: Animation` - A spring animation with a predefined duration and higher amount of   bounce. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/animation/bouncy))
- **default** `static let `default`: Animation` - A default animation instance. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/animation/default))
- **phaseAnimator(_:content:animation:)** `func phaseAnimator<Phase>(some Sequence, content: (PlaceholderContentView<Self>, Phase) -> some View, animation: (Phase) -> Animation?) -> some View` - Animates effects that you apply to a view over a sequence of phases   that change continuously. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/phaseanimator(_:content:animation:)))
- **phaseAnimator(_:trigger:content:animation:)** `func phaseAnimator<Phase>(some Sequence, trigger: some Equatable, content: (PlaceholderContentView<Self>, Phase) -> some View, animation: (Phase) -> Animation?) -> some View` - Animates effects that you apply to a view over a sequence of phases   that change based on a trigger. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/phaseanimator(_:trigger:content:animation:)))
- **withAnimation(_:_:)** `func withAnimation<Result>(Animation?, () throws -> Result) rethrows -> Result` - Returns the result of recomputing the view’s body with the provided   animation. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/withanimation(_:_:)))
- **withAnimation(_:completionCriteria:_:completion:)** `func withAnimation<Result>(Animation?, completionCriteria: AnimationCompletionCriteria, () throws -> Result, completion: () -> Void) rethrows -> Result` - Returns the result of recomputing the view’s body with the provided   animation, and runs the completion when all animations are complete. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/withanimation(_:completioncriteria:_:completion:)))

## App structure

- **AlertScene** `struct AlertScene` - A scene that renders itself as a standalone alert dialog. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/alertscene))
- **App** `protocol App` - A type that represents the structure and behavior of an app. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/app))
- **DefaultWindowVisibilityToggleLabel** `struct DefaultWindowVisibilityToggleLabel` - The default label of a window visibility toggle. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/defaultwindowvisibilitytogglelabel))
- **DismissWindowAction** `struct DismissWindowAction` - An action that dismisses a window associated to a particular scene. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/dismisswindowaction))
- **DocumentGroupLaunchScene** `struct DocumentGroupLaunchScene` - A launch scene for document-based applications. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/documentgrouplaunchscene))
- **MaterialActiveAppearance** `struct MaterialActiveAppearance` - The behavior for how materials appear active and inactive. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/materialactiveappearance))
- **NSApplicationDelegateAdaptor** `struct NSApplicationDelegateAdaptor` - A property wrapper type that you use to create an AppKit app delegate. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/nsapplicationdelegateadaptor))
- **NSHostingSceneBridgingOptions** `struct NSHostingSceneBridgingOptions` - Options for how hosting views and controllers manage aspects of the   associated window. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/nshostingscenebridgingoptions))
- **OpenWindowAction** `struct OpenWindowAction` - An action that presents a window. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/openwindowaction))
- **PresentedWindowContent** `struct PresentedWindowContent` - A view that represents the content of a presented window. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/presentedwindowcontent))
- **Preview(_:windowStyle:traits:body:)** `macro Preview<Style>(String?, windowStyle: Style, traits: PreviewTrait<Preview.ViewTraits>..., body: () -> any View)` - Creates a preview of a SwiftUI view in a window. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/preview(_:windowstyle:traits:body:)))
- **Preview(_:windowStyle:traits:body:cameras:)** `macro Preview<Style>(String?, windowStyle: Style, traits: PreviewTrait<Preview.ViewTraits>..., body: () -> any View, cameras: () -> [PreviewCamera])` - Creates a preview of a SwiftUI view in a window with custom viewpoints. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/preview(_:windowstyle:traits:body:cameras:)))
- **PushWindowAction** `struct PushWindowAction` - An action that opens the requested window in place of the window the action   is called from. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/pushwindowaction))
- **Scene** `protocol Scene` - A part of an app’s user interface with a life cycle managed by the   system. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scene))
- **SceneBuilder** `struct SceneBuilder` - A result builder for composing a collection of scenes into a single   composite scene. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scenebuilder))
- **SceneLaunchBehavior** `struct SceneLaunchBehavior` - The launch behavior for a scene. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scenelaunchbehavior))
- **ScenePadding** `struct ScenePadding` - The padding used to space a view from its containing scene. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scenepadding))
- **ScenePhase** `enum ScenePhase` - An indication of a scene’s operational state. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scenephase))
- **SceneRestorationBehavior** `struct SceneRestorationBehavior` - The restoration behavior for a scene. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scenerestorationbehavior))
- **SurfaceSnappingInfo** `struct SurfaceSnappingInfo` - A type representing information about the window scenes snap state. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/surfacesnappinginfo))
- **UIApplicationDelegateAdaptor** `struct UIApplicationDelegateAdaptor` - A property wrapper type that you use to create a UIKit app delegate. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/uiapplicationdelegateadaptor))
- **UIHostingSceneDelegate** `protocol UIHostingSceneDelegate` - Extends  UIKit/UISceneDelegate  to bridge SwiftUI scenes. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/uihostingscenedelegate))
- **UtilityWindow** `struct UtilityWindow` - A specialized window scene that provides secondary utility to the content   of the main scenes of an application. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/utilitywindow))
- **WKApplicationDelegateAdaptor** `struct WKApplicationDelegateAdaptor` - A property wrapper that is used in  App  to provide a delegate from   WatchKit. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/wkapplicationdelegateadaptor))
- **WKNotificationScene** `struct WKNotificationScene` - A scene which appears in response to receiving the specified   category of remote or local notifications. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/wknotificationscene))
- **Window** `struct Window` - A scene that presents its content in a single, unique window. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/window))
- **WindowGroup** `struct WindowGroup` - A scene that presents a group of identically structured windows. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/windowgroup))
- **WindowIdealSize** `struct WindowIdealSize` - A type which defines the size a window should use when zooming. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/windowidealsize))
- **WindowInteractionBehavior** `struct WindowInteractionBehavior` - Options for enabling and disabling window interaction behaviors. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/windowinteractionbehavior))
- **WindowLevel** `struct WindowLevel` - The level of a window. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/windowlevel))
- **WindowManagerRole** `struct WindowManagerRole` - Options for defining how a scene’s windows behave when used within a managed   window context, such as full screen mode and Stage Manager. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/windowmanagerrole))
- **WindowPlacement** `struct WindowPlacement` - A type which represents a preferred size and position for a window. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/windowplacement))
- **WindowProxy** `struct WindowProxy` - The proxy for an open window in the app. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/windowproxy))
- **WindowResizability** `struct WindowResizability` - The resizability of a window. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/windowresizability))
- **WindowStyle** `protocol WindowStyle` - A specification for the appearance and interaction of a window. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/windowstyle))
- **WindowVisibilityToggle** `struct WindowVisibilityToggle` - A specialized button for toggling the visibility of a window. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/windowvisibilitytoggle))
- **allowsWindowActivationEvents()** `func allowsWindowActivationEvents() -> some View` - Configures gestures in this view hierarchy to handle events that   activate the containing window. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/allowswindowactivationevents()))
- **allowsWindowActivationEvents(_:)** `func allowsWindowActivationEvents(Bool?) -> some View` - Configures whether gestures in this view hierarchy can handle events   that activate the containing window. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/allowswindowactivationevents(_:)))
- **appStoreMerchandising(isPresented:kind:onDismiss:)** `func appStoreMerchandising(isPresented: Binding<Bool>, kind: AppStoreMerchandisingKind, onDismiss: ((Result<AppStoreMerchandisingKind.PresentationResult, any Error>) async -> ())?) -> some View` ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/appstoremerchandising(ispresented:kind:ondismiss:)))
- **appStoreOverlay(isPresented:configuration:)** `func appStoreOverlay(isPresented: Binding<Bool>, configuration: () -> SKOverlay.Configuration) -> some View` - Presents a StoreKit overlay when a given condition is true. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/appstoreoverlay(ispresented:configuration:)))
- **backgroundTask(_:action:)** `func backgroundTask<D, R>(BackgroundTask<D, R>, action: (D) async -> R) -> some Scene` - Runs the specified action when the system provides a background task. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scene/backgroundtask(_:action:)))
- **chartForegroundStyleScale(domain:mapping:)** `func chartForegroundStyleScale<Domain, S>(domain: Domain, mapping: (Domain.Element) -> S) -> some View` - Configures the foreground style scale for charts. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/chartforegroundstylescale(domain:mapping:)))
- **chartForegroundStyleScale(mapping:)** `func chartForegroundStyleScale<DataValue, S>(mapping: (DataValue) -> S) -> some View` - Configures the foreground style scale for charts. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/chartforegroundstylescale(mapping:)))
- **chartLineStyleScale(domain:mapping:)** `func chartLineStyleScale<Domain>(domain: Domain, mapping: (Domain.Element) -> StrokeStyle) -> some View` - Configures the line style scale for charts. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/chartlinestylescale(domain:mapping:)))
- **chartLineStyleScale(mapping:)** `func chartLineStyleScale<DataValue>(mapping: (DataValue) -> StrokeStyle) -> some View` - Configures the line style scale for charts. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/chartlinestylescale(mapping:)))
- **chartSymbolScale(domain:mapping:)** `func chartSymbolScale<Domain, S>(domain: Domain, mapping: (Domain.Element) -> S) -> some View` - Configures the symbol scale for charts. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/chartsymbolscale(domain:mapping:)))
- **chartSymbolScale(mapping:)** `func chartSymbolScale<DataValue, S>(mapping: (DataValue) -> S) -> some View` - Configures the symbol scale for charts. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/chartsymbolscale(mapping:)))
- **chartSymbolSizeScale(domain:mapping:)** `func chartSymbolSizeScale<Domain>(domain: Domain, mapping: (Domain.Element) -> CGFloat) -> some View` - Configures the symbol size scale for charts. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/chartsymbolsizescale(domain:mapping:)))
- **chartSymbolSizeScale(mapping:)** `func chartSymbolSizeScale<DataValue>(mapping: (DataValue) -> CGFloat) -> some View` - Configures the symbol size scale for charts. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/chartsymbolsizescale(mapping:)))
- **commands(content:)** `func commands<Content>(content: () -> Content) -> some Scene` - Adds commands to the scene. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scene/commands(content:)))
- **commandsRemoved()** `func commandsRemoved() -> some Scene` - Removes all commands defined by the modified scene. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scene/commandsremoved()))
- **commandsReplaced(content:)** `func commandsReplaced<Content>(content: () -> Content) -> some Scene` - Replaces all commands defined by the modified scene with the commands   from the builder. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scene/commandsreplaced(content:)))
- **defaultLaunchBehavior(_:)** `func defaultLaunchBehavior(SceneLaunchBehavior) -> some Scene` - Sets the default launch behavior for this scene. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scene/defaultlaunchbehavior(_:)))
- **defaultPosition(_:)** `func defaultPosition(UnitPoint) -> some Scene` - Sets a default position for a window. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scene/defaultposition(_:)))
- **defaultSize(_:)** `func defaultSize(_:)` - Sets a default size for a window. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scene/defaultsize(_:)))
- **defaultSize(_:in:)** `func defaultSize(Size3D, in: UnitLength) -> some Scene` - Sets a default size for a volumetric window. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scene/defaultsize(_:in:)))
- **defaultSize(width:height:)** `func defaultSize(width: CGFloat, height: CGFloat) -> some Scene` - Sets a default width and height for a window. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scene/defaultsize(width:height:)))
- **defaultSize(width:height:depth:)** `func defaultSize(width: CGFloat, height: CGFloat, depth: CGFloat) -> some Scene` - Sets a default size for a volumetric window. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scene/defaultsize(width:height:depth:)))
- **defaultSize(width:height:depth:in:)** `func defaultSize(width: CGFloat, height: CGFloat, depth: CGFloat, in: UnitLength) -> some Scene` - Sets a default size for a volumetric window. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scene/defaultsize(width:height:depth:in:)))
- **defaultWindowPlacement(_:)** `func defaultWindowPlacement((WindowLayoutRoot, WindowPlacementContext) -> WindowPlacement) -> some Scene` - Defines a function used for determining the default placement   of windows. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scene/defaultwindowplacement(_:)))
- **defaultWorldScaling(_:)** `func defaultWorldScaling(WorldScalingBehavior) -> some Scene` - Specify the world scaling behavior for the window. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scene/defaultworldscaling(_:)))
- **dialogIcon(_:)** `func dialogIcon(Image?) -> some Scene` - Configures the icon used by alerts. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scene/dialogicon(_:)))
- **dialogPreventsAppTermination(_:)** `func dialogPreventsAppTermination(Bool?) -> some View` - Whether the alert or confirmation dialog prevents the app   from being quit/terminated by the system or app termination menu item. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/dialogpreventsapptermination(_:)))
- **dialogSeverity(_:)** `func dialogSeverity(DialogSeverity) -> some Scene` - Sets the severity for alerts. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scene/dialogseverity(_:)))
- **dialogSuppressionToggle(_:isSuppressed:)** `func dialogSuppressionToggle(_:isSuppressed:)` - Enables user suppression of an alert with a custom suppression   message. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scene/dialogsuppressiontoggle(_:issuppressed:)))
- **dialogSuppressionToggle(isSuppressed:)** `func dialogSuppressionToggle(isSuppressed: Binding<Bool>) -> some Scene` - Enables user suppression of an alert with a custom suppression   message. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scene/dialogsuppressiontoggle(issuppressed:)))
- **fileWrapper(configuration:)** `func fileWrapper(configuration: Self.WriteConfiguration) throws -> FileWrapper` - Serializes a document snapshot to a file wrapper. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/filedocument/filewrapper(configuration:)))
- **focusedSceneObject(_:)** `func focusedSceneObject(_:)` - Creates a new view that exposes the provided object to other views whose   whose state depends on the active scene. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/focusedsceneobject(_:)))
- **focusedSceneValue(_:)** `func focusedSceneValue<T>(T?) -> some View` - Sets the focused value for the given object type at a scene-wide scope. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/focusedscenevalue(_:)))
- **focusedSceneValue(_:_:)** `func focusedSceneValue(_:_:)` - Modifies this view by injecting a value that you provide for use by   other views whose state depends on the focused scene. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/focusedscenevalue(_:_:)))
- **handlesExternalEvents(matching:)** `func handlesExternalEvents(matching: Set<String>) -> some Scene` - Specifies the external events for which SwiftUI opens a new instance   of the modified scene. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scene/handlesexternalevents(matching:)))
- **immersionStyle(selection:in:)** `func immersionStyle(selection: Binding<any ImmersionStyle>, in: any ImmersionStyle...) -> some Scene` - Sets the style for an immersive space. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scene/immersionstyle(selection:in:)))
- **immersiveContentBrightness(_:)** `func immersiveContentBrightness(ImmersiveContentBrightness) -> some Scene` - Sets the content brightness of an immersive space. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scene/immersivecontentbrightness(_:)))
- **inAppPurchaseOptions(_:)** `func inAppPurchaseOptions(((Product) async -> Set<Product.PurchaseOption>)?) -> some View` - Add a function to call before initiating a purchase from StoreKit view within this view, providing a set   of options for the purchase. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/inapppurchaseoptions(_:)))
- **init(_:_:background:)** `init(some StringProtocol, () -> Actions, background: () -> some View)` - Creates a launch scene for document-based applications with a title, a set of actions, and a background. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/documentgrouplaunchscene/init(_:_:background:)-2iefz))

([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/documentgrouplaunchscene/init(_:_:background:backgroundaccessoryview:overlayaccessoryview:)-1re6d))

([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/documentgrouplaunchscene/init(_:_:background:overlayaccessoryview:)-1143c))

([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/documentgrouplaunchscene/init(_:backgroundstyle:_:backgroundaccessoryview:overlayaccessoryview:)-2d13c))
- **keyboardShortcut(_:)** `func keyboardShortcut(KeyboardShortcut?) -> some Scene` - Defines a keyboard shortcut for opening new scene windows. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scene/keyboardshortcut(_:)))
- **materialActiveAppearance(_:)** `func materialActiveAppearance(MaterialActiveAppearance) -> some View` - Sets an explicit active appearance for materials in this view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/materialactiveappearance(_:)))
- **menuBarExtraStyle(_:)** `func menuBarExtraStyle<S>(S) -> some Scene` - Sets the style for menu bar extra created by this scene. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scene/menubarextrastyle(_:)))
- **onAppIntentExecution(_:perform:)** `func onAppIntentExecution<I>(I.Type, perform: (I) -> Void) -> some View` - Registers a handler to invoke in response to the specified app intent that your   app receives. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/onappintentexecution(_:perform:)))
- **onAppear(perform:)** `func onAppear(perform: (() -> Void)?) -> some View` - Adds an action to perform before this view appears. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/onappear(perform:)))
- **onApplePayCouponCodeChange(perform:)** `func onApplePayCouponCodeChange(perform: (String) async -> PKPaymentRequestCouponCodeUpdate) -> some View` - Called when a user has entered or updated a coupon code.   This is required if the user is being asked to provide a coupon code. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/onapplepaycouponcodechange(perform:)))
- **onApplePayPaymentMethodChange(perform:)** `func onApplePayPaymentMethodChange(perform: (PKPaymentMethod) async -> PKPaymentRequestPaymentMethodUpdate) -> some View` - Called when a payment method has changed and asks for an update payment request.   If this modifier isn’t provided Wallet will assume the payment method is valid. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/onapplepaypaymentmethodchange(perform:)))
- **onApplePayShippingContactChange(perform:)** `func onApplePayShippingContactChange(perform: (PKContact) async -> PKPaymentRequestShippingContactUpdate) -> some View` - Called when a user selected a shipping address.   This is required if the user is being asked to provide a shipping contact. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/onapplepayshippingcontactchange(perform:)))
- **onApplePayShippingMethodChange(perform:)** `func onApplePayShippingMethodChange(perform: (PKShippingMethod) async -> PKPaymentRequestShippingMethodUpdate) -> some View` - Called when a user selected a shipping method.   This is required if the user is being asked to provide a shipping method. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/onapplepayshippingmethodchange(perform:)))
- **onDisappear(perform:)** `func onDisappear(perform: (() -> Void)?) -> some View` - Adds an action to perform after this view disappears. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/ondisappear(perform:)))
- **onInAppPurchaseCompletion(perform:)** `func onInAppPurchaseCompletion(perform: ((Product, Result<Product.PurchaseResult, any Error>) async -> ())?) -> some View` - Add an action to perform when a purchase initiated from a StoreKit view within this view completes. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/oninapppurchasecompletion(perform:)))
- **onInAppPurchaseStart(perform:)** `func onInAppPurchaseStart(perform: ((Product) async -> ())?) -> some View` - Add an action to perform when a user triggers the purchase button on a StoreKit view within this   view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/oninapppurchasestart(perform:)))

The  handler  closure takes a URL as input, and returns a  OpenURLAction.Result  that   indicates the outcome of the action. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/onopenurl(prefersinapp:)))

- **presentedWindowStyle(_:)** `func presentedWindowStyle<S>(S) -> some View` - Sets the style for windows created by interacting with this view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/presentedwindowstyle(_:)))

- **scenePadding(_:)** `func scenePadding(Edge.Set) -> some View` - Adds padding to the specified edges of this view using an amount that’s   appropriate for the current scene. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/scenepadding(_:)))
- **scenePadding(_:edges:)** `func scenePadding(ScenePadding, edges: Edge.Set) -> some View` - Adds a specified kind of padding to the specified edges of this view   using an amount that’s appropriate for the current scene. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/subscriptionintroductoryoffer(applyoffer:compactjws:)))
- **upperLimbVisibility(_:)** `func upperLimbVisibility(Visibility) -> some Scene` - Sets the preferred visibility of the user’s upper limbs, while an    scene is presented. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scene/upperlimbvisibility(_:)))
- **volumeWorldAlignment(_:)** `func volumeWorldAlignment(WorldAlignmentBehavior) -> some Scene` - Specifies how a volume should be aligned when moved in the world. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scene/volumeworldalignment(_:)))
- **windowBackgroundDragBehavior(_:)** `func windowBackgroundDragBehavior(WindowInteractionBehavior) -> some Scene` - Configures the behavior of dragging a window by its background. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scene/windowbackgrounddragbehavior(_:)))
- **windowDismissBehavior(_:)** `func windowDismissBehavior(WindowInteractionBehavior) -> some View` - Configures the dismiss functionality for the window enclosing  self . ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/windowdismissbehavior(_:)))
- **windowFullScreenBehavior(_:)** `func windowFullScreenBehavior(WindowInteractionBehavior) -> some View` - Configures the full screen functionality for the window enclosing  self . ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/windowfullscreenbehavior(_:)))
- **windowIdealPlacement(_:)** `func windowIdealPlacement((WindowLayoutRoot, WindowPlacementContext) -> WindowPlacement) -> some Scene` - Provides a function which determines a placement to use when windows   of a scene zoom. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scene/windowidealplacement(_:)))
- **windowIdealSize(_:)** `func windowIdealSize(WindowIdealSize) -> some Scene` - Specifies how windows derived form this scene should determine their   size when zooming. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scene/windowidealsize(_:)))
- **windowLevel(_:)** `func windowLevel(WindowLevel) -> some Scene` - Sets the window level of this scene. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scene/windowlevel(_:)))
- **windowManagerRole(_:)** `func windowManagerRole(WindowManagerRole) -> some Scene` - Configures the role for windows derived from  self  when   participating in a managed window context, such as full screen or   Stage Manager. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scene/windowmanagerrole(_:)))
- **windowMinimizeBehavior(_:)** `func windowMinimizeBehavior(WindowInteractionBehavior) -> some View` - Configures the minimize functionality for the window enclosing  self . ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/windowminimizebehavior(_:)))
- **windowResizability(_:)** `func windowResizability(WindowResizability) -> some Scene` - Sets the kind of resizability to use for a window. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scene/windowresizability(_:)))
- **windowResizeAnchor(_:)** `func windowResizeAnchor(UnitPoint?) -> some View` - Sets the window anchor point used when the size of the view   changes such that the window must resize. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/windowresizeanchor(_:)))
- **windowResizeBehavior(_:)** `func windowResizeBehavior(WindowInteractionBehavior) -> some View` - Configures the resize functionality for the window enclosing  self . ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/windowresizebehavior(_:)))
- **windowStyle(_:)** `func windowStyle<S>(S) -> some Scene` - Sets the style for windows created by this scene. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scene/windowstyle(_:)))
- **wrappedValue** `var wrappedValue: Value` - The underlying value referenced by the state variable. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/state/wrappedvalue))

## Controls and indicators

- **Button** `struct Button` - A control that initiates an action. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/button))
- **ButtonRepeatBehavior** `struct ButtonRepeatBehavior` - The options for controlling the repeatability of button actions. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/buttonrepeatbehavior))
- **ButtonRole** `struct ButtonRole` - A value that describes the purpose of a button. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/buttonrole))
- **ButtonSizing** `struct ButtonSizing` - The sizing behavior of  Button s and other button-like controls. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/buttonsizing))
- **ButtonStyle** `protocol ButtonStyle` - A type that applies standard interaction behavior and a custom appearance to   all buttons within a view hierarchy. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/buttonstyle))
- **ButtonStyleConfiguration** `struct ButtonStyleConfiguration` - The properties of a button. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/buttonstyleconfiguration))
- **ButtonStyleConfiguration.Label** `struct Label` - A type-erased label of a button. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/buttonstyleconfiguration/label-swift.struct))
- **ColorPicker** `struct ColorPicker` - A control used to select a color from the system color picker UI. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/colorpicker))
- **ControlActiveState** `enum ControlActiveState` - The active appearance expected of controls in a window. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/controlactivestate))
- **ControlGroup** `struct ControlGroup` - A container view that displays semantically-related controls   in a visually-appropriate manner for the context ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/controlgroup))
- **ControlGroupStyle** `protocol ControlGroupStyle` - Defines the implementation of all control groups within a view   hierarchy. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/controlgroupstyle))
- **ControlGroupStyleConfiguration** `struct ControlGroupStyleConfiguration` - The properties of a control group. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/controlgroupstyleconfiguration))
- **ControlGroupStyleConfiguration.Content** `struct Content` - A type-erased content of a  ControlGroup . ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/controlgroupstyleconfiguration/content-swift.struct))
- **ControlGroupStyleConfiguration.Label** `struct Label` - A type-erased label of a  . ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/controlgroupstyleconfiguration/label-swift.struct))
- **ControlSize** `enum ControlSize` - The size classes, like regular or small, that you can apply to controls   within a view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/controlsize))
- **ControlWidget** `protocol ControlWidget` - The configuration and content of a control widget to display in system spaces   such as Control Center, the Lock Screen, and the Action Button. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/controlwidget))
- **ControlWidgetConfiguration** `protocol ControlWidgetConfiguration` - A type that describes a control widget’s content. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/controlwidgetconfiguration))
- **ControlWidgetConfigurationBuilder** `struct ControlWidgetConfigurationBuilder` - A custom attribute that constructs a control widget’s body. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/controlwidgetconfigurationbuilder))
- **ControlWidgetTemplate** `protocol ControlWidgetTemplate` - A type that describes a control widget’s content. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/controlwidgettemplate))
- **ControlWidgetTemplateBuilder** `struct ControlWidgetTemplateBuilder` - A custom attribute that constructs a control widget template’s body. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/controlwidgettemplatebuilder))
- **DatePicker** `struct DatePicker` - A control for selecting an absolute date. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/datepicker))
- **DatePickerStyle** `protocol DatePickerStyle` - A type that specifies the appearance and interaction of all date pickers   within a view hierarchy. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/datepickerstyle))
- **DatePickerStyleConfiguration.Label** `struct Label` - A type-erased label of a  DatePicker . ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/datepickerstyleconfiguration/label-swift.struct))
- **DefaultButtonLabel** `struct DefaultButtonLabel` - The default label to use for a button. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/defaultbuttonlabel))
- **EditButton** `struct EditButton` - A button that toggles the edit mode environment value. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/editbutton))
- **EmptyControlWidgetConfiguration** `struct EmptyControlWidgetConfiguration` - An empty control widget configuration. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/emptycontrolwidgetconfiguration))
- **EmptyControlWidgetTemplate** `struct EmptyControlWidgetTemplate` - An empty control widget template. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/emptycontrolwidgettemplate))
- **GlassButtonStyle** `struct GlassButtonStyle` - A button style that applies glass border artwork based on the button’s   context. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/glassbuttonstyle))
- **GlassProminentButtonStyle** `struct GlassProminentButtonStyle` - A button style that applies prominent glass border artwork based on the button’s   context. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/glassprominentbuttonstyle))
- **LabeledControlGroupContent** `struct LabeledControlGroupContent` - A view that represents the body of a control group with a specified   label. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/labeledcontrolgroupcontent))
- **MenuButton** `struct MenuButton` - A button that displays a menu containing a list of choices when pressed. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/menubutton))
- **MultiDatePicker** `struct MultiDatePicker` - A control for picking multiple dates. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/multidatepicker))
- **NSHostingController** `class NSHostingController` - An AppKit view controller that hosts SwiftUI view hierarchy. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/nshostingcontroller))
- **NewDocumentButton** `struct NewDocumentButton` - A button that creates and opens new documents. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/newdocumentbutton))
- **PasteButton** `struct PasteButton` - A system button that reads items from the pasteboard and delivers it to a   closure. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/pastebutton))
- **Picker** `struct Picker` - A control for selecting from a set of mutually exclusive values. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/picker))
- **PickerStyle** `protocol PickerStyle` - A type that specifies the appearance and interaction of all pickers within   a view hierarchy. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/pickerstyle))
- **PrimitiveButtonStyle** `protocol PrimitiveButtonStyle` - A type that applies custom interaction behavior and a custom appearance to   all buttons within a view hierarchy. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/primitivebuttonstyle))
- **PrimitiveButtonStyleConfiguration** `struct PrimitiveButtonStyleConfiguration` - The properties of a button. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/primitivebuttonstyleconfiguration))
- **PrimitiveButtonStyleConfiguration.Label** `struct Label` - A type-erased label of a button. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/primitivebuttonstyleconfiguration/label-swift.struct))
- **PullDownButton** `typealias PullDownButton` ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/pulldownbutton))
- **RenameButton** `struct RenameButton` - A button that triggers a standard rename action. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/renamebutton))
- **TextInputFormattingControlPlacement** `struct TextInputFormattingControlPlacement` - A structure defining the system text formatting controls   available on each platform. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/textinputformattingcontrolplacement))
- **UIHostingController** `class UIHostingController` - A UIKit view controller that manages a SwiftUI view hierarchy. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/uihostingcontroller))
- **UIHostingControllerSizingOptions** `struct UIHostingControllerSizingOptions` - Options for how a hosting controller tracks its content’s size. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/uihostingcontrollersizingoptions))
- **WKHostingController** `class WKHostingController` - A WatchKit interface controller that hosts a SwiftUI view hierarchy. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/wkhostingcontroller))
- **WKUserNotificationHostingController** `class WKUserNotificationHostingController` - A WatchKit user notification interface controller that hosts a SwiftUI view   hierarchy. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/wkusernotificationhostingcontroller))
