## Drawing and graphics

- **GraphicsContext** `struct GraphicsContext` - An immediate mode drawing destination, and its current state. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/graphicscontext))
- **contentTransitionAddsDrawingGroup** `var contentTransitionAddsDrawingGroup: Bool` - A Boolean value that controls whether views that render content   transitions use GPU-accelerated rendering. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/contenttransitionaddsdrawinggroup))
- **drawingGroup(opaque:colorMode:)** `func drawingGroup(opaque: Bool, colorMode: ColorRenderingMode) -> some View` - Composites this view’s contents into an offscreen image before final   display. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/drawinggroup(opaque:colormode:)))

## Environment values

- **Environment** `struct Environment` - A property wrapper that reads a value from a view’s environment. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environment))
- **EnvironmentKey** `protocol EnvironmentKey` - A key for accessing values in the environment. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentkey))
- **EnvironmentObject** `struct EnvironmentObject` - A property wrapper type for an observable object that a parent or ancestor   view supplies. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentobject))
- **EnvironmentValues** `struct EnvironmentValues` - A collection of environment values propagated through a view hierarchy. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues))
- **EnvironmentalModifier** `protocol EnvironmentalModifier` - A modifier that must resolve to a concrete modifier in an environment before   use. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentalmodifier))
- **ImmersiveEnvironmentBehavior** `struct ImmersiveEnvironmentBehavior` - The behavior of the system-provided immersive environments when a scene is   opened by your app. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/immersiveenvironmentbehavior))
- **UITraitBridgedEnvironmentKey** `typealias UITraitBridgedEnvironmentKey` ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/uitraitbridgedenvironmentkey))
- **activityFamily** `var activityFamily: ActivityFamily` - The size family of the current Live Activity. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/activityfamily))
- **allowsTightening** `var allowsTightening: Bool` - A Boolean value that indicates whether inter-character spacing should   tighten to fit the text into the available space. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/allowstightening))
- **autocorrectionDisabled** `var autocorrectionDisabled: Bool` - A Boolean value that determines whether the view hierarchy has   auto-correction enabled. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/autocorrectiondisabled))
- **backgroundMaterial** `var backgroundMaterial: Material?` - The material underneath the current view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/backgroundmaterial))
- **backgroundProminence** `var backgroundProminence: BackgroundProminence` - The prominence of the background underneath views associated with this   environment. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/backgroundprominence))
- **backgroundStyle** `var backgroundStyle: AnyShapeStyle?` - An optional style that overrides the default system background   style when set. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/backgroundstyle))
- **badgeProminence** `var badgeProminence: BadgeProminence` - The prominence to apply to badges associated with this environment. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/badgeprominence))
- **calendar** `var calendar: Calendar` - The current calendar that views should use when handling dates. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/calendar))
- **colorScheme** `var colorScheme: ColorScheme` - The color scheme of this environment. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/colorscheme))
- **colorSchemeContrast** `var colorSchemeContrast: ColorSchemeContrast` - The contrast associated with the color scheme of this environment. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/colorschemecontrast))
- **contentTransition** `var contentTransition: ContentTransition` - The current method of animating the contents of views. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/contenttransition))
- **dismiss** `var dismiss: DismissAction` - An action that dismisses the current presentation. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/dismiss))
- **dismissImmersiveSpace** `var dismissImmersiveSpace: DismissImmersiveSpaceAction` - An immersive space dismissal action stored in a view’s environment. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/dismissimmersivespace))
- **dismissWindow** `var dismissWindow: DismissWindowAction` - A window dismissal action stored in a view’s environment. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/dismisswindow))
- **displayScale** `var displayScale: CGFloat` - The display scale of this environment. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/displayscale))
- **documentConfiguration** `var documentConfiguration: DocumentConfiguration?` - The configuration of a document in a  . ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/documentconfiguration))
- **dynamicTypeSize** `var dynamicTypeSize: DynamicTypeSize` - The current Dynamic Type size. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/dynamictypesize))
- **editMode** `var editMode: Binding<EditMode>?` - An indication of whether the user can edit the contents of a view   associated with this environment. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/editmode))
- **environment(_:)** `func environment<T>(T?) -> some Scene` - Places an observable object in the scene’s environment. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scene/environment(_:)))
- **environment(_:)** `func environment<T>(T?) -> some View` - Places an observable object in the view’s environment. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/environment(_:)))
- **environment(_:_:)** `func environment<V>(WritableKeyPath<EnvironmentValues, V>, V) -> some Scene` - Sets the environment value of the specified key path to the given value. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scene/environment(_:_:)))
- **environment(_:_:)** `func environment<V>(WritableKeyPath<EnvironmentValues, V>, V) -> some View` - Sets the environment value of the specified key path to the given value. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/environment(_:_:)))
- **environmentObject(_:)** `func environmentObject<T>(T) -> some Scene` - Supplies an  ObservableObject  to a view subhierarchy. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scene/environmentobject(_:)))
- **environmentObject(_:)** `func environmentObject<T>(T) -> some View` - Supplies an observable object to a view’s hierarchy. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/environmentobject(_:)))
- **font** `var font: Font?` - The default font of this environment. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/font))
- **headerProminence** `var headerProminence: Prominence` - The prominence to apply to section headers within a view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/headerprominence))
- **horizontalSizeClass** `var horizontalSizeClass: UserInterfaceSizeClass?` - The horizontal size class of this environment. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/horizontalsizeclass))
- **immersiveSpaceDisplacement** `var immersiveSpaceDisplacement: Pose3D` - The displacement that the system applies to the immersive space when   moving the space away from its default position, in meters. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/immersivespacedisplacement))
- **isActivityFullscreen** `var isActivityFullscreen: Bool` - A Boolean value that indicates whether the Live Activity appears in a   full-screen presentation. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/isactivityfullscreen))
- **isEnabled** `var isEnabled: Bool` - A Boolean value that indicates whether the view associated with this   environment allows user interaction. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/isenabled))
- **isFocusEffectEnabled** `var isFocusEffectEnabled: Bool` - A Boolean value that indicates whether the view associated with this   environment allows focus effects to be displayed. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/isfocuseffectenabled))
- **isFocused** `var isFocused: Bool` - Returns whether the nearest focusable ancestor has focus. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/isfocused))
- **isHoverEffectEnabled** `var isHoverEffectEnabled: Bool` - A Boolean value that indicates whether the view associated with this   environment allows hover effects to be displayed. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/ishovereffectenabled))
- **isLuminanceReduced** `var isLuminanceReduced: Bool` - A Boolean value that indicates whether the display or environment currently requires   reduced luminance. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/isluminancereduced))
- **isPresented** `var isPresented: Bool` - A Boolean value that indicates whether the view associated with this   environment is currently presented. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/ispresented))
- **isSceneCaptured** `var isSceneCaptured: Bool` - The current capture state. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/isscenecaptured))
- **isTabBarShowingSections** `var isTabBarShowingSections: Bool` - A Boolean value that determines whether a tab view shows the   expanded contents of a tab section. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/istabbarshowingsections))
- **keyboardShortcut** `var keyboardShortcut: KeyboardShortcut?` - The keyboard shortcut that buttons in this environment will be triggered   with. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/keyboardshortcut))
- **labelsVisibility** `var labelsVisibility: Visibility` - The labels visibility set by  . ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/labelsvisibility))
- **legibilityWeight** `var legibilityWeight: LegibilityWeight?` - The font weight to apply to text. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/legibilityweight))
- **lineLimit** `var lineLimit: Int?` - The maximum number of lines that text can occupy in a view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/linelimit))
- **lineSpacing** `var lineSpacing: CGFloat` - The distance in points between the bottom of one line fragment and the   top of the next. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/linespacing))
- **locale** `var locale: Locale` - The current locale that views should use. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/locale))
- **materialActiveAppearance** `var materialActiveAppearance: MaterialActiveAppearance` - The behavior materials should use for their active state, defaulting to   automatic . ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/materialactiveappearance))
- **menuIndicatorVisibility** `var menuIndicatorVisibility: Visibility` - The menu indicator visibility to apply to controls within a view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/menuindicatorvisibility))
- **menuOrder** `var menuOrder: MenuOrder` - The preferred order of items for menus presented from this view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/menuorder))
- **minimumScaleFactor** `var minimumScaleFactor: CGFloat` - The minimum permissible proportion to shrink the font size to fit   the text into the available space. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/minimumscalefactor))
- **newDocument** `var newDocument: NewDocumentAction` - An action in the environment that presents a new document. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/newdocument))
- **openDocument** `var openDocument: OpenDocumentAction` - An action in the environment that presents an existing document. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/opendocument))
- **openImmersiveSpace** `var openImmersiveSpace: OpenImmersiveSpaceAction` - An action that presents an immersive space. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/openimmersivespace))
- **openSettings** `var openSettings: OpenSettingsAction` - A Settings presentation action stored in a view’s environment. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/opensettings))
- **openURL** `var openURL: OpenURLAction` - An action that opens a URL. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/openurl))
- **openWindow** `var openWindow: OpenWindowAction` - A window presentation action stored in a view’s environment. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/openwindow))
- **pixelLength** `var pixelLength: CGFloat` - The size of a pixel on the screen. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/pixellength))
- **preferredPencilDoubleTapAction** `var preferredPencilDoubleTapAction: PencilPreferredAction` - The action that the user prefers to perform after double-tapping their   Apple Pencil, as selected in the Settings app. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/preferredpencildoubletapaction))
- **preferredPencilSqueezeAction** `var preferredPencilSqueezeAction: PencilPreferredAction` - The action that the user prefers to perform when squeezing their Apple   Pencil, as selected in the Settings app. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/preferredpencilsqueezeaction))
- **redactionReasons** `var redactionReasons: RedactionReasons` - The current redaction reasons applied to the view hierarchy. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/redactionreasons))
- **refresh** `var refresh: RefreshAction?` - A refresh action stored in a view’s environment. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/refresh))
- **rename** `var rename: RenameAction?` - An action that activates the standard rename interaction. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/rename))
- **resetFocus** `var resetFocus: ResetFocusAction` - An action that requests the focus system to reevaluate default focus. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/resetfocus))
- **scenePhase** `var scenePhase: ScenePhase` - The current phase of the scene. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/scenephase))
- **sidebarRowSize** `var sidebarRowSize: SidebarRowSize` - The current size of sidebar rows. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/sidebarrowsize))
- **springLoadingBehavior** `var springLoadingBehavior: SpringLoadingBehavior` - The behavior of spring loaded interactions for the views associated   with this environment. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/springloadingbehavior))
- **supportsMultipleWindows** `var supportsMultipleWindows: Bool` - A Boolean value that indicates whether the current platform supports   opening multiple windows. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/supportsmultiplewindows))
- **symbolRenderingMode** `var symbolRenderingMode: SymbolRenderingMode?` - The current symbol rendering mode, or  nil  denoting that the   mode is picked automatically using the current image and   foreground style as parameters. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/symbolrenderingmode))
- **symbolVariants** `var symbolVariants: SymbolVariants` - The symbol variant to use in this environment. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/symbolvariants))
- **tabBarPlacement** `var tabBarPlacement: TabBarPlacement?` - The current placement of the tab bar. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/tabbarplacement))
- **timeZone** `var timeZone: TimeZone` - The current time zone that views should use when handling dates. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/timezone))
- **transformEnvironment(_:transform:)** `func transformEnvironment<V>(WritableKeyPath<EnvironmentValues, V>, transform: (inout V) -> Void) -> some Scene` - Transforms the environment value of the specified key path with the   given function. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scene/transformenvironment(_:transform:)))
- **transformEnvironment(_:transform:)** `func transformEnvironment<V>(WritableKeyPath<EnvironmentValues, V>, transform: (inout V) -> Void) -> some View` - Transforms the environment value of the specified key path with the   given function. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/transformenvironment(_:transform:)))
- **truncationMode** `var truncationMode: Text.TruncationMode` - A value that indicates how the layout truncates the last line of text to   fit into the available space. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/truncationmode))
- **undoManager** `var undoManager: UndoManager?` - The undo manager used to register a view’s undo operations. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/undomanager))
- **verticalSizeClass** `var verticalSizeClass: UserInterfaceSizeClass?` - The vertical size class of this environment. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/verticalsizeclass))
- **webAuthenticationSession** `var webAuthenticationSession: WebAuthenticationSession` - A value provided in the SwiftUI environment that views can use to   authenticate a user through a web service. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/webauthenticationsession))
- **worldTrackingLimitations** `var worldTrackingLimitations: Set<WorldTrackingLimitation>` - The current limitations of the device tracking the user’s surroundings. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/worldtrackinglimitations))

## Framework integration

- **mapCameraKeyframeAnimator(trigger:keyframes:)** `func mapCameraKeyframeAnimator(trigger: some Equatable, keyframes: (MapCamera) -> some Keyframes<MapCamera>) -> some View` - Uses the given keyframes to animate the camera of a  Map  when the   given trigger value changes. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/mapcamerakeyframeanimator(trigger:keyframes:)))
- **mapFeatureSelectionAccessory(_:)** `func mapFeatureSelectionAccessory(MapItemDetailSelectionAccessoryStyle?) -> some View` - Specifies the selection accessory to display for a  MapFeature ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/mapfeatureselectionaccessory(_:)))
- **mapFeatureSelectionContent(content:)** `func mapFeatureSelectionContent(content: (MapFeature) -> some MapContent) -> some View` - Specifies a custom presentation for the currently selected feature. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/mapfeatureselectioncontent(content:)))
- **mapFeatureSelectionDisabled(_:)** `func mapFeatureSelectionDisabled((MapFeature) -> Bool) -> some View` - Specifies which map features should have selection disabled. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/mapfeatureselectiondisabled(_:)))
- **mapItemDetailPopover(isPresented:item:displaysMap:attachmentAnchor:)** `func mapItemDetailPopover(isPresented: Binding<Bool>, item: MKMapItem?, displaysMap: Bool, attachmentAnchor: PopoverAttachmentAnchor) -> some View` - Presents a map item detail popover. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/mapitemdetailpopover(ispresented:item:displaysmap:attachmentanchor:)))
- **mapItemDetailPopover(isPresented:item:displaysMap:attachmentAnchor:arrowEdge:)** `func mapItemDetailPopover(isPresented: Binding<Bool>, item: MKMapItem?, displaysMap: Bool, attachmentAnchor: PopoverAttachmentAnchor, arrowEdge: Edge) -> some View` - Presents a map item detail popover. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/mapitemdetailpopover(ispresented:item:displaysmap:attachmentanchor:arrowedge:)))
- **mapItemDetailPopover(item:displaysMap:attachmentAnchor:)** `func mapItemDetailPopover(item: Binding<MKMapItem?>, displaysMap: Bool, attachmentAnchor: PopoverAttachmentAnchor) -> some View` - Presents a map item detail popover. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/mapitemdetailpopover(item:displaysmap:attachmentanchor:)))
- **mapItemDetailPopover(item:displaysMap:attachmentAnchor:arrowEdge:)** `func mapItemDetailPopover(item: Binding<MKMapItem?>, displaysMap: Bool, attachmentAnchor: PopoverAttachmentAnchor, arrowEdge: Edge) -> some View` - Presents a map item detail popover. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/mapitemdetailpopover(item:displaysmap:attachmentanchor:arrowedge:)))
- **mapItemDetailSheet(isPresented:item:displaysMap:)** `func mapItemDetailSheet(isPresented: Binding<Bool>, item: MKMapItem?, displaysMap: Bool) -> some View` - Presents a map item detail sheet. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/mapitemdetailsheet(ispresented:item:displaysmap:)))
- **mapItemDetailSheet(item:displaysMap:)** `func mapItemDetailSheet(item: Binding<MKMapItem?>, displaysMap: Bool) -> some View` - Presents a map item detail sheet. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/mapitemdetailsheet(item:displaysmap:)))
- **mapScope(_:)** `func mapScope(Namespace.ID) -> some View` - Creates a mapScope that SwiftUI uses to connect map controls to an associated map. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/mapscope(_:)))
- **mapStyle(_:)** `func mapStyle(MapStyle) -> some View` - Specifies the map style to be used. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/mapstyle(_:)))
- **onMapCameraChange(frequency:_:)** `func onMapCameraChange(frequency:_:)` - Performs an action when Map camera framing changes ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/onmapcamerachange(frequency:_:)))

## Gestures

- **AnyGesture** `struct AnyGesture` - A type-erased gesture. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/anygesture))
- **DragGesture** `struct DragGesture` - A dragging motion that invokes an action as the drag-event sequence changes. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/draggesture))
- **ExclusiveGesture** `struct ExclusiveGesture` - A gesture that consists of two gestures where only one of them can succeed. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/exclusivegesture))
- **Gesture** `protocol Gesture` - An instance that matches a sequence of events to a gesture, and returns a   stream of values for each of its states. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/gesture))
- **GestureMask** `struct GestureMask` - Options that control how adding a gesture to a view affects other gestures   recognized by the view and its subviews. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/gesturemask))
- **GestureState** `struct GestureState` - A property wrapper type that updates a property while the user performs a   gesture and resets the property back to its initial state when the gesture   ends. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/gesturestate))
- **GestureStateGesture** `struct GestureStateGesture` - A gesture that updates the state provided by a gesture’s updating callback. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/gesturestategesture))
- **HandGestureShortcut** `struct HandGestureShortcut` - Hand gesture shortcuts describe finger and wrist movements that the user can   perform in order to activate a button or toggle. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/handgestureshortcut))
- **LongPressGesture** `struct LongPressGesture` - A gesture that succeeds when the user performs a long press. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/longpressgesture))
- **MagnificationGesture** `struct MagnificationGesture` - A gesture that recognizes a magnification motion and tracks the amount of   magnification. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/magnificationgesture))
- **MagnifyGesture** `struct MagnifyGesture` - A gesture that recognizes a magnification motion and tracks the amount of   magnification. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/magnifygesture))
- **ManipulationGestureModifier** `struct ManipulationGestureModifier` ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/manipulationgesturemodifier))
- **ManipulationUsingGestureStateModifier** `struct ManipulationUsingGestureStateModifier` ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/manipulationusinggesturestatemodifier))
- **NSGestureRecognizerRepresentable** `protocol NSGestureRecognizerRepresentable` - A wrapper for an  NSGestureRecognizer  that you use to integrate that   gesture recognizer into your SwiftUI hierarchy. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/nsgesturerecognizerrepresentable))
- **NSGestureRecognizerRepresentableContext** `struct NSGestureRecognizerRepresentableContext` - Contextual information about the state of the system that you use to create   and update a represented gesture recognizer. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/nsgesturerecognizerrepresentablecontext))
- **NSGestureRecognizerRepresentableCoordinateSpaceConverter** `struct NSGestureRecognizerRepresentableCoordinateSpaceConverter` - A structure used to convert locations to and from coordinate spaces in the   hierarchy of the SwiftUI view associated with an   . ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/nsgesturerecognizerrepresentablecoordinatespaceconverter))
- **PencilDoubleTapGestureValue** `struct PencilDoubleTapGestureValue` - Describes the value of an Apple Pencil double-tap gesture. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/pencildoubletapgesturevalue))
- **PencilSqueezeGesturePhase** `enum PencilSqueezeGesturePhase` - Describes the phase and value of an Apple Pencil squeeze gesture. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/pencilsqueezegesturephase))
- **PencilSqueezeGestureValue** `struct PencilSqueezeGestureValue` - Describes the value of an Apple Pencil squeeze gesture. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/pencilsqueezegesturevalue))
- **RotateGesture** `struct RotateGesture` - A gesture that recognizes a rotation motion and tracks the angle of the   rotation. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/rotategesture))
- **RotateGesture3D** `struct RotateGesture3D` - A gesture that recognizes 3D rotation motion and tracks the angle and axis   of the rotation. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/rotategesture3d))
- **RotationGesture** `struct RotationGesture` - A gesture that recognizes a rotation motion and tracks the angle of the rotation. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/rotationgesture))
- **SequenceGesture** `struct SequenceGesture` - A gesture that’s a sequence of two gestures. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/sequencegesture))
- **SimultaneousGesture** `struct SimultaneousGesture` - A gesture containing two gestures that can happen at the same time with   neither of them preceding the other. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/simultaneousgesture))
- **SpatialEventGesture** `struct SpatialEventGesture` - A gesture that provides information about ongoing spatial events like   clicks and touches. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/spatialeventgesture))
- **SpatialTapGesture** `struct SpatialTapGesture` - A gesture that recognizes one or more taps and reports their location. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/spatialtapgesture))
- **TapGesture** `struct TapGesture` - A gesture that recognizes one or more taps. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/tapgesture))
- **UIGestureRecognizerRepresentable** `protocol UIGestureRecognizerRepresentable` - A wrapper for a  UIGestureRecognizer  that you use to integrate that gesture   recognizer into your SwiftUI hierarchy. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/uigesturerecognizerrepresentable))
- **UIGestureRecognizerRepresentableContext** `struct UIGestureRecognizerRepresentableContext` - Contextual information about the state of the system that you use to create   and update a represented gesture recognizer. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/uigesturerecognizerrepresentablecontext))
- **UIGestureRecognizerRepresentableCoordinateSpaceConverter** `struct UIGestureRecognizerRepresentableCoordinateSpaceConverter` - A proxy structure used to convert locations to/from coordinate spaces in the   hierarchy of the SwiftUI view associated with a   . ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/uigesturerecognizerrepresentablecoordinatespaceconverter))
- **WindowDragGesture** `struct WindowDragGesture` - A gesture that recognizes the motion of and handles dragging a window. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/windowdraggesture))
- **chartGesture(_:)** `func chartGesture((ChartProxy) -> some Gesture) -> some View` ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/chartgesture(_:)))
- **defersSystemGestures(on:)** `func defersSystemGestures(on: Edge.Set) -> some View` - Sets the screen edge from which you want your gesture to take   precedence over the system gesture. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/deferssystemgestures(on:)))
- **exclusively(before:)** `func exclusively<Other>(before: Other) -> ExclusiveGesture<Self, Other>` - Combines two gestures exclusively to create a new gesture where only one   gesture succeeds, giving precedence to the first gesture. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/gesture/exclusively(before:)))
- **gesture(_:)** `func gesture(_:)` - Attaches an   to the view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/gesture(_:)))
- **gesture(_:including:)** `func gesture<T>(T, including: GestureMask) -> some View` - Attaches a gesture to the view with a lower precedence than gestures   defined by the view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/gesture(_:including:)))
- **gesture(_:isEnabled:)** `func gesture<T>(T, isEnabled: Bool) -> some View` - Attaches a gesture to the view with a lower precedence than gestures   defined by the view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/gesture(_:isenabled:)))
- **gesture(_:name:isEnabled:)** `func gesture<T>(T, name: String, isEnabled: Bool) -> some View` - Attaches a gesture to the view with a lower precedence than gestures   defined by the view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/gesture(_:name:isenabled:)))
- **handGestureShortcut(_:isEnabled:)** `func handGestureShortcut(HandGestureShortcut, isEnabled: Bool) -> some View` - Assigns a hand gesture shortcut to the modified control. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/handgestureshortcut(_:isenabled:)))
- **highPriorityGesture(_:including:)** `func highPriorityGesture<T>(T, including: GestureMask) -> some View` - Attaches a gesture to the view with a higher precedence than gestures   defined by the view. - **highPriorityGesture(_:isEnabled:)** `func highPriorityGesture<T>(T, isEnabled: Bool) -> some View` - Attaches a gesture to the view with a higher precedence than gestures   defined by the view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/highprioritygesture(_:isenabled:)))
- **highPriorityGesture(_:name:isEnabled:)** `func highPriorityGesture<T>(T, name: String, isEnabled: Bool) -> some View` - Attaches a gesture to the view with a higher precedence than gestures   defined by the view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/highprioritygesture(_:name:isenabled:)))
- **manipulationGesture(updating:coordinateSpace:operations:inertia:isEnabled:onChanged:)** `func manipulationGesture(updating: Binding<Manipulable.GestureState>, coordinateSpace: some CoordinateSpaceProtocol, operations: Manipulable.Operation.Set, inertia: Manipulable.Inertia, isEnabled: Bool, onChanged: ((Manipulable.Event) -> Void)?) -> some View` - Adds a manipulation gesture to this view without allowing this view   to be manipulable itself. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/manipulationgesture(updating:coordinatespace:operations:inertia:isenabled:onchanged:)))
- **maximumDistance** `var maximumDistance: CGFloat` - The maximum distance that the long press can move before the gesture   fails. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/longpressgesture/maximumdistance))
- **minimumDuration** `var minimumDuration: Double` - The minimum duration of the long press that must elapse before the   gesture succeeds. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/longpressgesture/minimumduration))

- **onChanged(_:)** `func onChanged((Self.Value) -> Void) -> _ChangedGesture<Self>` - Adds an action to perform when the gesture’s value changes. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/gesture/onchanged(_:)))
- **onEnded(_:)** `func onEnded((Self.Value) -> Void) -> _EndedGesture<Self>` - Adds an action to perform when the gesture ends. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/gesture/onended(_:)))

- **onLongPressGesture(minimumDuration:pressing:perform:)** `func onLongPressGesture(minimumDuration: Double, pressing: ((Bool) -> Void)?, perform: () -> Void) -> some View` - Adds an action to perform when this view recognizes a long press   gesture. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/onlongpressgesture(minimumduration:pressing:perform:)))
- **onLongTouchGesture(minimumDuration:perform:onTouchingChanged:)** `func onLongTouchGesture(minimumDuration: Double, perform: () -> Void, onTouchingChanged: ((Bool) -> Void)?) -> some View` - Adds an action to perform when this view recognizes a   remote long touch gesture. A long touch gesture is when the finger is   on the remote touch surface without actually pressing. - **onTapGesture(count:coordinateSpace:perform:)** `func onTapGesture(count:coordinateSpace:perform:)` - Adds an action to perform when this view recognizes a tap gesture,   and provides the action with the location of the interaction. - **onTapGesture(count:perform:)** `func onTapGesture(count: Int, perform: () -> Void) -> some View` - Adds an action to perform when this view recognizes a tap gesture. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/ontapgesture(count:perform:)))
- **sequenced(before:)** `func sequenced<Other>(before: Other) -> SequenceGesture<Self, Other>` - Sequences a gesture with another one to create a new gesture, which   results in the second gesture only receiving events after the first   gesture succeeds. - **simultaneousGesture(_:including:)** `func simultaneousGesture<T>(T, including: GestureMask) -> some View` - Attaches a gesture to the view to process simultaneously with gestures   defined by the view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/simultaneousgesture(_:including:)))
- **simultaneousGesture(_:isEnabled:)** `func simultaneousGesture<T>(T, isEnabled: Bool) -> some View` - Attaches a gesture to the view to process simultaneously with gestures   defined by the view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/simultaneousgesture(_:isenabled:)))
- **simultaneousGesture(_:name:isEnabled:)** `func simultaneousGesture<T>(T, name: String, isEnabled: Bool) -> some View` - Attaches a gesture to the view to process simultaneously with gestures   defined by the view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/simultaneousgesture(_:name:isenabled:)))

## Images

- **AsyncImage** `struct AsyncImage` - A view that asynchronously loads and displays an image. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/asyncimage))
- **AsyncImagePhase** `enum AsyncImagePhase` - The current phase of the asynchronous image loading operation. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/asyncimagephase))
- **Image** `struct Image` - A view that displays an image. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/image))
- **Image.Interpolation.medium** `case medium` - A value that indicates a medium level of interpolation quality,   between the low- and high-quality values. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/image/interpolation/medium))
- **Image.Interpolation.none** `case none` - A value that indicates SwiftUI doesn’t interpolate image data. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/image/interpolation/none))
- **Image.Orientation** `enum Orientation` - The orientation of an image. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/image/orientation))
- **Image.ResizingMode** `enum ResizingMode` - The modes that SwiftUI uses to resize an image to fit within   its containing view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/image/resizingmode))
- **Image.ResizingMode.tile** `case tile` - A mode to repeat the image at its original size, as many   times as necessary to fill the available space. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/image/resizingmode/tile))
- **Image.Scale** `enum Scale` - A scale to apply to vector images relative to text. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/image/scale))
- **ImageRenderer** `class ImageRenderer` - An object that creates images from SwiftUI views. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/imagerenderer))
- **imagePlaygroundGenerationStyle(_:in:)** `func imagePlaygroundGenerationStyle(ImagePlaygroundStyle, in: [ImagePlaygroundStyle]) -> some View` - Sets the generation style for an image playground. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/imageplaygroundgenerationstyle(_:in:)))
- **imagePlaygroundOptions(_:)** `func imagePlaygroundOptions(ImagePlaygroundOptions) -> some View` - Options influencing image generation ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/imageplaygroundoptions(_:)))
- **imagePlaygroundPersonalizationPolicy(_:)** `func imagePlaygroundPersonalizationPolicy(ImagePlaygroundPersonalizationPolicy) -> some View` - Policy determining whether to support the usage of people in the playground or not. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/imageplaygroundpersonalizationpolicy(_:)))
- **imagePlaygroundSheet(isPresented:concept:sourceImage:onCompletion:onCancellation:)** `func imagePlaygroundSheet(isPresented: Binding<Bool>, concept: String, sourceImage: Image?, onCompletion: (URL) -> Void, onCancellation: (() -> Void)?) -> some View` - Presents the system sheet to create images from the   specified input. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/imageplaygroundsheet(ispresented:concept:sourceimage:oncompletion:oncancellation:)))
- **imagePlaygroundSheet(isPresented:concept:sourceImageURL:onCompletion:onCancellation:)** `func imagePlaygroundSheet(isPresented: Binding<Bool>, concept: String, sourceImageURL: URL, onCompletion: (URL) -> Void, onCancellation: (() -> Void)?) -> some View` - Presents the system sheet to create images from the specified input. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/imageplaygroundsheet(ispresented:concept:sourceimageurl:oncompletion:oncancellation:)))
- **imagePlaygroundSheet(isPresented:concepts:sourceImage:onCompletion:onCancellation:)** `func imagePlaygroundSheet(isPresented: Binding<Bool>, concepts: [ImagePlaygroundConcept], sourceImage: Image?, onCompletion: (URL) -> Void, onCancellation: (() -> Void)?) -> some View` - Presents the system sheet to create images from the   specified input. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/imageplaygroundsheet(ispresented:concepts:sourceimage:oncompletion:oncancellation:)))
- **imagePlaygroundSheet(isPresented:concepts:sourceImageURL:onCompletion:onCancellation:)** `func imagePlaygroundSheet(isPresented: Binding<Bool>, concepts: [ImagePlaygroundConcept], sourceImageURL: URL, onCompletion: (URL) -> Void, onCancellation: (() -> Void)?) -> some View` - Presents the system sheet to create images from the   specified input. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/imageplaygroundsheet(ispresented:concepts:sourceimageurl:oncompletion:oncancellation:)))
- **imageScale** `var imageScale: Image.Scale` - The image scale for this environment. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/imagescale))
- **imageScale(_:)** `func imageScale(Image.Scale) -> some View` - Scales images within the view according to one of the relative sizes   available including small, medium, and large images sizes. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/imagescale(_:)))
- **init(_:systemImage:value:content:)** `init(_:systemImage:value:content:)` - Creates a tab that the tab view presents when the tab view’s selection   matches the tab’s value using a system image for the tab’s tab item image,   with a localized string key label. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/tab/init(_:systemimage:value:content:)))
- **interpolation(_:)** `func interpolation(Image.Interpolation) -> Image` - Specifies the current level of quality for rendering an   image that requires interpolation. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/image/interpolation(_:)))
- **resizable(capInsets:resizingMode:)** `func resizable(capInsets: EdgeInsets, resizingMode: Image.ResizingMode) -> Image` - Sets the mode by which SwiftUI resizes an image to fit its space. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/image/resizable(capinsets:resizingmode:)))
- **tipImageSize(_:)** `func tipImageSize(CGSize) -> some View` - Sets the size for a tip’s image. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/tipimagesize(_:)))
- **tipImageStyle(_:)** `func tipImageStyle<S>(S) -> some View` - Sets the style for a tip’s image. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/tipimagestyle(_:)))
- **tipImageStyle(_:_:)** `func tipImageStyle<S1, S2>(S1, S2) -> some View` - Sets the style for a tip’s image. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/tipimagestyle(_:_:)))
- **tipImageStyle(_:_:_:)** `func tipImageStyle<S1, S2, S3>(S1, S2, S3) -> some View` - Sets the style for a tip’s image. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/tipimagestyle(_:_:_:)))

## Layout

- **AnyLayout** `struct AnyLayout` - A type-erased instance of the layout protocol. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/anylayout))
- **GridLayout** `struct GridLayout` - A grid that you can use in conditional layouts. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/gridlayout))
- **HStackLayout** `struct HStackLayout` - A horizontal container that you can use in conditional layouts. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/hstacklayout))
- **Layout** `protocol Layout` - A type that defines the geometry of a collection of views. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/layout))
- **LayoutDirection** `enum LayoutDirection` - A direction in which SwiftUI can lay out content. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/layoutdirection))
- **LayoutDirectionBehavior** `enum LayoutDirectionBehavior` - A description of what should happen when the layout direction changes. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/layoutdirectionbehavior))
- **LayoutProperties** `struct LayoutProperties` - Layout-specific properties of a layout container. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/layoutproperties))
- **LayoutRotationUnaryLayout** `struct LayoutRotationUnaryLayout` ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/layoutrotationunarylayout))
- **LayoutSubview** `struct LayoutSubview` - A proxy that represents one subview of a layout. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/layoutsubview))
- **LayoutSubviews** `struct LayoutSubviews` - A collection of proxy values that represent the subviews of a layout view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/layoutsubviews))
- **LayoutValueKey** `protocol LayoutValueKey` - A key for accessing a layout value of a layout container’s subviews. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/layoutvaluekey))
- **VStackLayout** `struct VStackLayout` - A vertical container that you can use in conditional layouts. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/vstacklayout))
- **WindowLayoutRoot** `struct WindowLayoutRoot` - A proxy which represents the root contents of a window. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/windowlayoutroot))
- **ZStackLayout** `struct ZStackLayout` - An overlaying container that you can use in conditional layouts. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/zstacklayout))
- **flipsForRightToLeftLayoutDirection(_:)** `func flipsForRightToLeftLayoutDirection(Bool) -> some View` - Sets whether this view mirrors its contents horizontally when the layout   direction is right-to-left. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/flipsforrighttoleftlayoutdirection(_:)))
- **horizontalRadioGroupLayout()** `func horizontalRadioGroupLayout() -> some View` - Sets the style for radio group style pickers within this view to be   horizontally positioned with the radio buttons inside the layout. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/horizontalradiogrouplayout()))
- **layoutDirection** `var layoutDirection: LayoutDirection` - The layout direction associated with the current environment. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/layoutdirection))
- **layoutDirectionBehavior(_:)** `func layoutDirectionBehavior(LayoutDirectionBehavior) -> some View` - Sets the behavior of this view for different layout directions. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/layoutdirectionbehavior(_:)))
- **layoutPriority(_:)** `func layoutPriority(Double) -> some View` - Sets the priority by which a parent layout should apportion space to   this child. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/layoutpriority(_:)))
- **layoutValue(key:value:)** `func layoutValue<K>(key: K.Type, value: K.Value) -> some View` - Associates a value with a custom layout property. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/layoutvalue(key:value:)))
- **makeCache(subviews:)** `func makeCache(subviews: Self.Subviews) -> Self.Cache` - Creates and initializes a cache for a layout instance. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/layout/makecache(subviews:)))
- **placeSubviews(in:proposal:subviews:cache:)** `func placeSubviews(in: CGRect, proposal: ProposedViewSize, subviews: Self.Subviews, cache: inout Self.Cache)` - Assigns positions to each of the layout’s subviews. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/layout/placesubviews(in:proposal:subviews:cache:)))
- **previewLayout(_:)** `func previewLayout(PreviewLayout) -> some View` - Overrides the size of the container for the preview. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/previewlayout(_:)))
- **realityViewLayoutBehavior(_:)** `func realityViewLayoutBehavior(RealityViewLayoutOption) -> some View` - A view modifier that controls the frame sizing and content alignment behavior for  RealityView ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/realityviewlayoutbehavior(_:)))
- **rotation3DLayout(_:)** `func rotation3DLayout(Rotation3D) -> some View` - Rotates a view with impacts to its frame in a containing layout ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/rotation3dlayout(_:)))
- **rotation3DLayout(_:axis:)** `func rotation3DLayout(_:axis:)` - Rotates a view with impacts to its frame in a containing layout ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/rotation3dlayout(_:axis:)))
- **scrollTargetLayout(isEnabled:)** `func scrollTargetLayout(isEnabled: Bool) -> some View` - Configures the outermost layout as a scroll target layout. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/scrolltargetlayout(isenabled:)))
- **sizeThatFits(proposal:subviews:cache:)** `func sizeThatFits(proposal: ProposedViewSize, subviews: Self.Subviews, cache: inout Self.Cache) -> CGSize` - Returns the size of the composite view, given a proposed size   and the view’s subviews. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/layout/sizethatfits(proposal:subviews:cache:)))
- **updateCache(_:subviews:)** `func updateCache(inout Self.Cache, subviews: Self.Subviews)` - Updates the layout’s cache when something changes. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/layout/updatecache(_:subviews:)))

## Lists

- **List** `struct List` - A container that presents rows of data arranged in a single column,   optionally providing the ability to select one or more members. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/list))
- **ListItemTint** `struct ListItemTint` - A tint effect configuration that you can apply to content in a list. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/listitemtint))
- **ListSectionSpacing** `struct ListSectionSpacing` - The spacing options between two adjacent sections in a list. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/listsectionspacing))
- **ListStyle** `protocol ListStyle` - A protocol that describes the behavior and appearance of a list. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/liststyle))
- **defaultMinListHeaderHeight** `var defaultMinListHeaderHeight: CGFloat?` - The default minimum height of a header in a list. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/defaultminlistheaderheight))
- **defaultMinListRowHeight** `var defaultMinListRowHeight: CGFloat` - The default minimum height of rows in a list. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/defaultminlistrowheight))
- **listItemTint(_:)** `func listItemTint(_:)` - Sets a fixed tint color for content in a list. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/listitemtint(_:)))
- **listRowBackground(_:)** `func listRowBackground<V>(V?) -> some View` - Places a custom background view behind a list row item. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/listrowbackground(_:)))
- **listRowHoverEffect(_:)** `func listRowHoverEffect(HoverEffect?) -> some View` - Requests that the containing list row use the provided hover effect. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/listrowhovereffect(_:)))
- **listRowHoverEffectDisabled(_:)** `func listRowHoverEffectDisabled(Bool) -> some View` - Requests that the containing list row have its hover effect disabled. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/listrowhovereffectdisabled(_:)))
- **listRowInsets(_:)** `func listRowInsets(EdgeInsets?) -> some View` - Applies an inset to the rows in a list. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/listrowinsets(_:)))
- **listRowInsets(_:_:)** `func listRowInsets(Edge.Set, CGFloat?) -> some View` - Sets the insets of rows in a list on the specified edges. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/listrowinsets(_:_:)))
- **listRowPlatterColor(_:)** `func listRowPlatterColor(Color?) -> some View` - Sets the color that the system applies to the row background when this   view is placed in a list. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/listrowplattercolor(_:)))
- **listRowSeparator(_:edges:)** `func listRowSeparator(Visibility, edges: VerticalEdge.Set) -> some View` - Sets the display mode for the separator associated with this specific row. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/listrowseparator(_:edges:)))
- **listRowSeparatorTint(_:edges:)** `func listRowSeparatorTint(Color?, edges: VerticalEdge.Set) -> some View` - Sets the tint color associated with a row. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/listrowseparatortint(_:edges:)))
- **listRowSpacing(_:)** `func listRowSpacing(CGFloat?) -> some View` - Sets the vertical spacing between two adjacent rows in a List. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/listrowspacing(_:)))
- **listSectionIndexVisibility(_:)** `func listSectionIndexVisibility(Visibility) -> some View` - Changes the visibility of the list section index. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/listsectionindexvisibility(_:)))
- **listSectionMargins(_:_:)** `func listSectionMargins(Edge.Set, CGFloat?) -> some View` - Set the section margins for the specific edges. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/listsectionmargins(_:_:)))
- **listSectionSeparator(_:edges:)** `func listSectionSeparator(Visibility, edges: VerticalEdge.Set) -> some View` - Sets whether to hide the separator associated with a list section. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/listsectionseparator(_:edges:)))
- **listSectionSeparatorTint(_:edges:)** `func listSectionSeparatorTint(Color?, edges: VerticalEdge.Set) -> some View` - Sets the tint color associated with a section. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/listsectionseparatortint(_:edges:)))
- **listSectionSpacing(_:)** `func listSectionSpacing(_:)` - Sets the spacing between adjacent sections in a   to a custom   value. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/listsectionspacing(_:)))
- **listStyle(_:)** `func listStyle<S>(S) -> some View` - Sets the style for lists within this view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/liststyle(_:)))

## Navigation

- **NavigationBarItem** `struct NavigationBarItem` - A configuration for a navigation bar that represents a view at the top of a   navigation stack. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/navigationbaritem))
- **NavigationLink** `struct NavigationLink` - A view that controls a navigation presentation. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/navigationlink))
- **NavigationPath** `struct NavigationPath` - A type-erased list of data representing the content of a navigation stack. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/navigationpath))
- **NavigationSplitView** `struct NavigationSplitView` - A view that presents views in two or three columns, where selections in   leading columns control presentations in subsequent columns. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/navigationsplitview))
- **NavigationSplitViewColumn** `struct NavigationSplitViewColumn` - A view that represents a column in a navigation split view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/navigationsplitviewcolumn))
- **NavigationSplitViewStyle** `protocol NavigationSplitViewStyle` - A type that specifies the appearance and interaction of navigation split   views within a view hierarchy. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/navigationsplitviewstyle))
- **NavigationSplitViewVisibility** `struct NavigationSplitViewVisibility` - The visibility of the leading columns in a navigation split view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/navigationsplitviewvisibility))
- **NavigationStack** `struct NavigationStack` - A view that displays a root view and enables you to present additional   views over the root view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/navigationstack))
- **NavigationTransition** `protocol NavigationTransition` - A type that defines the transition to use when navigating to a view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/navigationtransition))
- **NavigationView** `struct NavigationView` - A view for presenting a stack of views that represents a visible path in a   navigation hierarchy. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/navigationview))
- **append(_:)** `func append(_:)` - Appends a new codable value to the end of this path. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/navigationpath/append(_:)))
- **assistiveAccessNavigationIcon(_:)** `func assistiveAccessNavigationIcon(Image) -> some View` - Configures the view’s icon for purposes of navigation. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/assistiveaccessnavigationicon(_:)))
- **assistiveAccessNavigationIcon(systemImage:)** `func assistiveAccessNavigationIcon(systemImage: String) -> some View` - Configures the view’s icon for purposes of navigation. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/assistiveaccessnavigationicon(systemimage:)))
- **init(_:value:)** `init(_:value:)` - Creates a navigation link that presents the view corresponding to a   codable value, with a text label that the link generates from a   localized string key. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/navigationlink/init(_:value:)))
- **init(destination:label:)** `init(destination:label:)` - Creates a navigation link that presents the destination view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/navigationlink/init(destination:label:)))
- **init(path:root:)** `init(path:root:)` - Creates a navigation stack with homogeneous navigation state that you   can control. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/navigationstack/init(path:root:)))
- **init(sidebar:content:detail:)** `init(sidebar: () -> Sidebar, content: () -> Content, detail: () -> Detail)` - Creates a three-column navigation split view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/navigationsplitview/init(sidebar:content:detail:)))
- **init(sidebar:detail:)** `init(sidebar: () -> Sidebar, detail: () -> Detail)` - Creates a two-column navigation split view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/navigationsplitview/init(sidebar:detail:)))
- **init(value:label:)** `init(value:label:)` - Creates a navigation link that presents the view corresponding to a   codable value. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/navigationlink/init(value:label:)))
- **lookAroundViewer(isPresented:initialScene:allowsNavigation:showsRoadLabels:pointsOfInterest:onDismiss:)** `func lookAroundViewer(isPresented: Binding<Bool>, initialScene: MKLookAroundScene?, allowsNavigation: Bool, showsRoadLabels: Bool, pointsOfInterest: PointOfInterestCategories, onDismiss: (() -> Void)?) -> some View` ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/lookaroundviewer(ispresented:initialscene:allowsnavigation:showsroadlabels:pointsofinterest:ondismiss:)))
- **lookAroundViewer(isPresented:scene:allowsNavigation:showsRoadLabels:pointsOfInterest:onDismiss:)** `func lookAroundViewer(isPresented: Binding<Bool>, scene: Binding<MKLookAroundScene?>, allowsNavigation: Bool, showsRoadLabels: Bool, pointsOfInterest: PointOfInterestCategories, onDismiss: (() -> Void)?) -> some View` ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/lookaroundviewer(ispresented:scene:allowsnavigation:showsroadlabels:pointsofinterest:ondismiss:)))
- **navigationBarBackButtonHidden(_:)** `func navigationBarBackButtonHidden(Bool) -> some View` - Hides the navigation bar back button for the view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/navigationbarbackbuttonhidden(_:)))
- **navigationBarHidden(_:)** `func navigationBarHidden(Bool) -> some View` - Hides the navigation bar for this view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/navigationbarhidden(_:)))
- **navigationBarItems(leading:)** `func navigationBarItems<L>(leading: L) -> some View` - Sets the navigation bar items for this view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/navigationbaritems(leading:)))
- **navigationBarItems(leading:trailing:)** `func navigationBarItems<L, T>(leading: L, trailing: T) -> some View` - Sets the navigation bar items for this view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/navigationbaritems(leading:trailing:)))
- **navigationBarItems(trailing:)** `func navigationBarItems<T>(trailing: T) -> some View` - Configures the navigation bar items for this view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/navigationbaritems(trailing:)))
- **navigationBarTitle(_:)** `func navigationBarTitle(_:)` - Sets the title in the navigation bar for this view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/navigationbartitle(_:)))
- **navigationBarTitle(_:displayMode:)** `func navigationBarTitle(_:displayMode:)` - Sets the title and display mode in the navigation bar for this view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/navigationbartitle(_:displaymode:)))
- **navigationBarTitleDisplayMode(_:)** `func navigationBarTitleDisplayMode(NavigationBarItem.TitleDisplayMode) -> some View` - Configures the title display mode for this view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/navigationbartitledisplaymode(_:)))
- **navigationDestination(for:destination:)** `func navigationDestination<D, C>(for: D.Type, destination: (D) -> C) -> some View` - Associates a destination view with a presented data type for use within   a navigation stack. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/navigationdestination(for:destination:)))
- **navigationDestination(isPresented:destination:)** `func navigationDestination<V>(isPresented: Binding<Bool>, destination: () -> V) -> some View` - Associates a destination view with a binding that can be used to push   the view onto a  . ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/navigationdestination(ispresented:destination:)))
- **navigationDestination(item:destination:)** `func navigationDestination<D, C>(item: Binding<Optional<D>>, destination: (D) -> C) -> some View` - Associates a destination view with a bound value for use within a   navigation stack or navigation split view ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/navigationdestination(item:destination:)))
- **navigationDocument(_:)** `func navigationDocument(_:)` - Configures the view’s document for purposes of navigation. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/navigationdocument(_:)))
- **navigationDocument(_:preview:)** `func navigationDocument(_:preview:)` - Configures the view’s document for purposes of navigation. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/navigationdocument(_:preview:)))
- **navigationLinkIndicatorVisibility(_:)** `func navigationLinkIndicatorVisibility(Visibility) -> some View` - Configures whether navigation links show a disclosure indicator. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/navigationlinkindicatorvisibility(_:)))
- **navigationSplitViewColumnWidth(_:)** `func navigationSplitViewColumnWidth(CGFloat) -> some View` - Sets a fixed, preferred width for the column containing this view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/navigationsplitviewcolumnwidth(_:)))
- **navigationSplitViewColumnWidth(min:ideal:max:)** `func navigationSplitViewColumnWidth(min: CGFloat?, ideal: CGFloat, max: CGFloat?) -> some View` - Sets a flexible, preferred width for the column containing this view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/navigationsplitviewcolumnwidth(min:ideal:max:)))
- **navigationSplitViewStyle(_:)** `func navigationSplitViewStyle<S>(S) -> some View` - Sets the style for navigation split views within this view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/navigationsplitviewstyle(_:)))
- **navigationSubtitle(_:)** `func navigationSubtitle(_:)` - Configures the view’s subtitle for purposes of navigation. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/navigationsubtitle(_:)))
- **navigationTitle(_:)** `func navigationTitle(_:)` - Configures the view’s title for purposes of navigation, using a string   binding. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/navigationtitle(_:)))
- **navigationTransition(_:)** `func navigationTransition(some NavigationTransition) -> some View` - Sets the navigation transition style for this view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/navigationtransition(_:)))
- **navigationViewStyle(_:)** `func navigationViewStyle<S>(S) -> some View` - Sets the style for navigation views within this view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/navigationviewstyle(_:)))
- **stack** `static var stack: StackNavigationViewStyle` - A navigation view style represented by a view stack that only shows a single   top view at a time. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/navigationviewstyle/stack))
- **webViewBackForwardNavigationGestures(_:)** `func webViewBackForwardNavigationGestures(WebView.BackForwardNavigationGesturesBehavior) -> some View` - Determines whether horizontal swipe gestures trigger backward and forward page navigation. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/webviewbackforwardnavigationgestures(_:)))
- **zoom(sourceID:in:)** `static func zoom(sourceID: some Hashable, in: Namespace.ID) -> ZoomNavigationTransition` - A navigation transition that zooms the appearing view from a   given source view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/navigationtransition/zoom(sourceid:in:)))

## Persistent storage

- **AppStorage** `struct AppStorage` - A property wrapper type that reflects a value from  UserDefaults  and   invalidates a view on a change in value in that user default. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/appstorage))
- **SceneStorage** `struct SceneStorage` - A property wrapper type that reads and writes to persisted, per-scene   storage. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scenestorage))
- **defaultAppStorage(_:)** `func defaultAppStorage(UserDefaults) -> some View` - The default store used by  AppStorage  contained within the view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/defaultappstorage(_:)))

## Preferences

- **PreferenceKey** `protocol PreferenceKey` - A named value produced by a view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/preferencekey))
- **anchorPreference(key:value:transform:)** `func anchorPreference<A, K>(key: K.Type, value: Anchor<A>.Source, transform: (Anchor<A>) -> K.Value) -> some View` - Sets a value for the specified preference key, the value is a   function of a geometry value tied to the current coordinate   space, allowing readers of the value to convert the geometry to   their local coordinates. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/anchorpreference(key:value:transform:)))
- **backgroundPreferenceValue(_:_:)** `func backgroundPreferenceValue<Key, T>(Key.Type, (Key.Value) -> T) -> some View` - Reads the specified preference value from the view, using it to   produce a second view that is applied as the background of the   original view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/backgroundpreferencevalue(_:_:)))
- **backgroundPreferenceValue(_:alignment:_:)** `func backgroundPreferenceValue<K, V>(K.Type, alignment: Alignment, (K.Value) -> V) -> some View` - Reads the specified preference value from the view, using it to   produce a second view that is applied as the background of the   original view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/backgroundpreferencevalue(_:alignment:_:)))
- **onPreferenceChange(_:perform:)** `func onPreferenceChange<K>(K.Type, perform: (K.Value) -> Void) -> some View` - Adds an action to perform when the specified preference key’s value   changes. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/onpreferencechange(_:perform:)))
- **overlayPreferenceValue(_:_:)** `func overlayPreferenceValue<Key, T>(Key.Type, (Key.Value) -> T) -> some View` - Reads the specified preference value from the view, using it to   produce a second view that is applied as an overlay to the   original view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/overlaypreferencevalue(_:_:)))
- **overlayPreferenceValue(_:alignment:_:)** `func overlayPreferenceValue<K, V>(K.Type, alignment: Alignment, (K.Value) -> V) -> some View` - Reads the specified preference value from the view, using it to   produce a second view that is applied as an overlay to the   original view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/overlaypreferencevalue(_:alignment:_:)))
- **preference(key:value:)** `func preference<K>(key: K.Type, value: K.Value) -> some View` - Sets a value for the given preference. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/preference(key:value:)))
- **spatialOverlayPreferenceValue(_:alignment:_:)** `func spatialOverlayPreferenceValue<K, V>(K.Type, alignment: Alignment3D, (K.Value) -> V) -> some View` - Uses the specified preference value from the view to produce another   view occupying the same 3D space of the first view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/spatialoverlaypreferencevalue(_:alignment:_:)))
- **task(id:name:executorPreference:priority:file:line:_:)** `func task<T>(id: T, name: String?, executorPreference: any TaskExecutor, priority: TaskPriority, file: String, line: Int, sending () async -> Void) -> some View` - Adds a task to perform before this view appears or when a specified   value changes. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/task(id:name:executorpreference:priority:file:line:_:)))
- **task(name:executorPreference:priority:file:line:action:)** `func task(name: String?, executorPreference: any TaskExecutor, priority: TaskPriority, file: String, line: Int, action: sending () async -> Void) -> some View` - Adds an asynchronous task to perform before this view appears. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/task(name:executorpreference:priority:file:line:action:)))
- **transformAnchorPreference(key:value:transform:)** `func transformAnchorPreference<A, K>(key: K.Type, value: Anchor<A>.Source, transform: (inout K.Value, Anchor<A>) -> Void) -> some View` - Sets a value for the specified preference key, the value is a   function of the key’s current value and a geometry value tied   to the current coordinate space, allowing readers of the value   to convert the geometry to their local coordinates. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/transformanchorpreference(key:value:transform:)))
- **transformPreference(_:_:)** `func transformPreference<K>(K.Type, (inout K.Value) -> Void) -> some View` - Applies a transformation to a preference value. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/transformpreference(_:_:)))

## Presentation

- **CustomPresentationDetent** `protocol CustomPresentationDetent` - The definition of a custom detent with a calculated height. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/custompresentationdetent))
- **NSHostingSceneRepresentation** `class NSHostingSceneRepresentation` - An AppKit type that hosts and can present SwiftUI scenes ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/nshostingscenerepresentation))
- **PresentationAdaptation** `struct PresentationAdaptation` - Strategies for adapting a presentation to a different size class. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/presentationadaptation))
- **PresentationBackgroundInteraction** `struct PresentationBackgroundInteraction` - The kinds of interaction available to views behind a presentation. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/presentationbackgroundinteraction))
- **PresentationContentInteraction** `struct PresentationContentInteraction` - A behavior that you can use to influence how a presentation responds to   swipe gestures. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/presentationcontentinteraction))
- **PresentationDetent** `struct PresentationDetent` - A type that represents a height where a sheet naturally rests. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/presentationdetent))
- **PresentationSizing** `protocol PresentationSizing` - A type that defines the size of the presentation content and how the   presentation size adjusts to its content’s size changing. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/presentationsizing))
- **PresentationSizingContext** `struct PresentationSizingContext` - Contextual information about a presentation. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/presentationsizingcontext))
- **PresentationSizingRoot** `struct PresentationSizingRoot` - A proxy to a view provided to the presentation with a   defined presentation size. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/presentationsizingroot))
- **SearchPresentationToolbarBehavior** `struct SearchPresentationToolbarBehavior` - A type that defines how the toolbar behaves when presenting search. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/searchpresentationtoolbarbehavior))
- **onSearchPresentation** `static var onSearchPresentation: SearchScopeActivation` - An activation where the system shows search scopes after   presenting search and hides search scopes after search   cancellation. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/searchscopeactivation/onsearchpresentation))
- **presentationBackground(_:)** `func presentationBackground<S>(S) -> some View` - Sets the presentation background of the enclosing sheet using a shape   style. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/presentationbackground(_:)))
- **presentationBackground(alignment:content:)** `func presentationBackground<V>(alignment: Alignment, content: () -> V) -> some View` - Sets the presentation background of the enclosing sheet to a custom   view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/presentationbackground(alignment:content:)))
- **presentationBackgroundInteraction(_:)** `func presentationBackgroundInteraction(PresentationBackgroundInteraction) -> some View` - Controls whether people can interact with the view behind a   presentation. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/presentationbackgroundinteraction(_:)))
- **presentationBreakthroughEffect(_:)** `func presentationBreakthroughEffect(BreakthroughEffect) -> some View` - Changes the way the enclosing presentation breaks through content   occluding it. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/presentationbreakthrougheffect(_:)))
- **presentationCompactAdaptation(_:)** `func presentationCompactAdaptation(PresentationAdaptation) -> some View` - Specifies how to adapt a presentation to compact size classes. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/presentationcompactadaptation(_:)))
- **presentationCompactAdaptation(horizontal:vertical:)** `func presentationCompactAdaptation(horizontal: PresentationAdaptation, vertical: PresentationAdaptation) -> some View` - Specifies how to adapt a presentation to horizontally and vertically   compact size classes. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/presentationcompactadaptation(horizontal:vertical:)))
- **presentationContentInteraction(_:)** `func presentationContentInteraction(PresentationContentInteraction) -> some View` - Configures the behavior of swipe gestures on a presentation. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/presentationcontentinteraction(_:)))
- **presentationCornerRadius(_:)** `func presentationCornerRadius(CGFloat?) -> some View` - Requests that the presentation have a specific corner radius. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/presentationcornerradius(_:)))
- **presentationDetents(_:)** `func presentationDetents(Set<PresentationDetent>) -> some View` - Sets the available detents for the enclosing sheet. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/presentationdetents(_:)))
- **presentationDetents(_:selection:)** `func presentationDetents(Set<PresentationDetent>, selection: Binding<PresentationDetent>) -> some View` - Sets the available detents for the enclosing sheet, giving you   programmatic control of the currently selected detent. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/presentationdetents(_:selection:)))
- **presentationDragIndicator(_:)** `func presentationDragIndicator(Visibility) -> some View` - Sets the visibility of the drag indicator on top of a sheet. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/presentationdragindicator(_:)))
- **presentationPreventsAppTermination(_:)** `func presentationPreventsAppTermination(Bool?) -> some View` - Whether a presentation prevents the app from being terminated/quit   by the system or app termination menu item. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/presentationpreventsapptermination(_:)))
- **presentationSizing(_:)** `func presentationSizing(some PresentationSizing) -> some View` - Sets the sizing of the containing presentation. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/presentationsizing(_:)))
- **searchPresentationToolbarBehavior(_:)** `func searchPresentationToolbarBehavior(SearchPresentationToolbarBehavior) -> some View` - Configures the search toolbar presentation behavior for any   searchable modifiers within this view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/searchpresentationtoolbarbehavior(_:)))
- **translationPresentation(isPresented:text:attachmentAnchor:arrowEdge:replacementAction:)** `func translationPresentation(isPresented: Binding<Bool>, text: String, attachmentAnchor: PopoverAttachmentAnchor, arrowEdge: Edge, replacementAction: ((String) -> Void)?) -> some View` - Presents a translation popover when a given condition is true.
