([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/translationpresentation(ispresented:text:attachmentanchor:arrowedge:replacementaction:)))

## Scroll views

- **AnyScrollTargetBehavior** `struct AnyScrollTargetBehavior` - A type-erased scroll target behavior. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/anyscrolltargetbehavior))
- **PagingScrollTargetBehavior** `struct PagingScrollTargetBehavior` - The scroll behavior that aligns scroll targets to container-based geometry. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/pagingscrolltargetbehavior))
- **PinnedScrollableViews** `struct PinnedScrollableViews` - A set of view types that may be pinned to the bounds of a scroll view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/pinnedscrollableviews))
- **ScrollAnchorRole** `struct ScrollAnchorRole` - A type defining the role of a scroll anchor. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scrollanchorrole))
- **ScrollBounceBehavior** `struct ScrollBounceBehavior` - The ways that a scrollable view can bounce when it reaches the end of its   content. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scrollbouncebehavior))
- **ScrollContentOffsetAdjustmentBehavior** `struct ScrollContentOffsetAdjustmentBehavior` - A type that defines the different kinds of content offset adjusting   behaviors a scroll view can have. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scrollcontentoffsetadjustmentbehavior))
- **ScrollDismissesKeyboardMode** `struct ScrollDismissesKeyboardMode` - The ways that scrollable content can interact with the software keyboard. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scrolldismisseskeyboardmode))
- **ScrollEdgeEffectStyle** `struct ScrollEdgeEffectStyle` - A structure that defines the style of pocket a scroll view will have. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scrolledgeeffectstyle))
- **ScrollGeometry** `struct ScrollGeometry` - A type that defines the geometry of a scroll view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scrollgeometry))
- **ScrollIndicatorVisibility** `struct ScrollIndicatorVisibility` - The visibility of scroll indicators of a UI element. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scrollindicatorvisibility))
- **ScrollInputBehavior** `struct ScrollInputBehavior` - A type that defines whether input should scroll a view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scrollinputbehavior))
- **ScrollInputKind** `struct ScrollInputKind` - Inputs used to scroll views. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scrollinputkind))
- **ScrollPhase** `enum ScrollPhase` - A type that describes the state of a scroll gesture of a   scrollable view like a scroll view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scrollphase))
- **ScrollPhaseChangeContext** `struct ScrollPhaseChangeContext` - A type that provides you with more content when the phase of a scroll   view changes. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scrollphasechangecontext))
- **ScrollPosition** `struct ScrollPosition` - A type that defines the semantic position of where a scroll view is   scrolled within its content. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scrollposition))
- **ScrollTarget** `struct ScrollTarget` - A type defining the target in which a scroll view should try and scroll to. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scrolltarget))
- **ScrollTargetBehavior** `protocol ScrollTargetBehavior` - A type that defines the scroll behavior of a scrollable view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scrolltargetbehavior))
- **ScrollTargetBehaviorContext** `struct ScrollTargetBehaviorContext` - The context in which a scroll target behavior updates its scroll target. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scrolltargetbehaviorcontext))
- **ScrollTargetBehaviorProperties** `struct ScrollTargetBehaviorProperties` - Properties influencing the scroll view a scroll target behavior   applies to. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scrolltargetbehaviorproperties))
- **ScrollTargetBehaviorPropertiesContext** `struct ScrollTargetBehaviorPropertiesContext` - The context in which a scroll target behavior can decide its properties. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scrolltargetbehaviorpropertiescontext))
- **ScrollTransitionConfiguration** `struct ScrollTransitionConfiguration` - The configuration of a scroll transition that controls how a transition   is applied as a view is scrolled through the visible region of a containing   scroll view or other container. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scrolltransitionconfiguration))
- **ScrollTransitionPhase** `enum ScrollTransitionPhase` - The phases that a view transitions between when it scrolls among other views. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scrolltransitionphase))
- **ScrollView** `struct ScrollView` - A scrollable view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scrollview))
- **ScrollViewProxy** `struct ScrollViewProxy` - A proxy value that supports programmatic scrolling of the scrollable   views within a view hierarchy. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scrollviewproxy))
- **ScrollViewReader** `struct ScrollViewReader` - A view that provides programmatic scrolling, by working with a proxy   to scroll to known child views. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scrollviewreader))
- **ViewAlignedScrollTargetBehavior** `struct ViewAlignedScrollTargetBehavior` - The scroll behavior that aligns scroll targets to view-based geometry. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/viewalignedscrolltargetbehavior))
- **chartScrollPosition(initialX:)** `func chartScrollPosition(initialX: some Plottable) -> some View` - Sets the initial scroll position along the x-axis.   Once the user scrolls the scroll view, the value provided to this   modifier will have no effect. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/chartscrollposition(initialx:)))
- **chartScrollPosition(initialY:)** `func chartScrollPosition(initialY: some Plottable) -> some View` - Sets the initial scroll position along the y-axis.   Once the user scrolls the scroll view, the value provided to this   modifier will have no effect. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/chartscrollposition(initialy:)))
- **chartScrollPosition(x:)** `func chartScrollPosition(x: Binding<some Plottable>) -> some View` - Associates a binding to be updated when the chart scrolls along the x-axis. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/chartscrollposition(x:)))
- **chartScrollPosition(y:)** `func chartScrollPosition(y: Binding<some Plottable>) -> some View` - Associates a binding to be updated when the chart scrolls along the y-axis. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/chartscrollposition(y:)))
- **chartScrollTargetBehavior(_:)** `func chartScrollTargetBehavior(some ChartScrollTargetBehavior) -> some View` - Sets the scroll behavior of the scrollable chart. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/chartscrolltargetbehavior(_:)))
- **chartScrollableAxes(_:)** `func chartScrollableAxes(Axis.Set) -> some View` - Configures the scrollable behavior of charts in this view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/chartscrollableaxes(_:)))
- **defaultScrollAnchor(_:)** `func defaultScrollAnchor(UnitPoint?) -> some View` - Associates an anchor to control which part of the scroll view’s   content should be rendered by default. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/defaultscrollanchor(_:)))
- **defaultScrollAnchor(_:for:)** `func defaultScrollAnchor(UnitPoint?, for: ScrollAnchorRole) -> some View` - Associates an anchor to control the position of a scroll view in a   particular circumstance. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/defaultscrollanchor(_:for:)))
- **horizontalScrollBounceBehavior** `var horizontalScrollBounceBehavior: ScrollBounceBehavior` - The scroll bounce mode for the horizontal axis of scrollable views. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/horizontalscrollbouncebehavior))
- **horizontalScrollIndicatorVisibility** `var horizontalScrollIndicatorVisibility: ScrollIndicatorVisibility` - The visibility to apply to scroll indicators of any   horizontally scrollable content. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/horizontalscrollindicatorvisibility))
- **isScrollEnabled** `var isScrollEnabled: Bool` - A Boolean value that indicates whether any scroll views associated   with this environment allow scrolling to occur. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/isscrollenabled))
- **onScrollGeometryChange(for:of:action:)** `func onScrollGeometryChange<T>(for: T.Type, of: (ScrollGeometry) -> T, action: (T, T) -> Void) -> some View` - Adds an action to be performed when a value, created from a   scroll geometry, changes. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/onscrollgeometrychange(for:of:action:)))
- **onScrollPhaseChange(_:)** `func onScrollPhaseChange(_:)` - Adds an action to perform when the scroll phase of the first scroll   view in the hierarchy changes. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/onscrollphasechange(_:)))
- **onScrollTargetVisibilityChange(idType:threshold:_:)** `func onScrollTargetVisibilityChange<ID>(idType: ID.Type, threshold: Double, ([ID]) -> Void) -> some View` - Adds an action to be called with information about what views would   be considered visible. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/onscrolltargetvisibilitychange(idtype:threshold:_:)))
- **onScrollVisibilityChange(threshold:_:)** `func onScrollVisibilityChange(threshold: Double, (Bool) -> Void) -> some View` - Adds an action to be called when the view crosses the threshold to be considered on/off screen. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/onscrollvisibilitychange(threshold:_:)))
- **scrollBounceBehavior(_:axes:)** `func scrollBounceBehavior(ScrollBounceBehavior, axes: Axis.Set) -> some View` - Configures the bounce behavior of scrollable views along the specified   axis. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/scrollbouncebehavior(_:axes:)))
- **scrollClipDisabled(_:)** `func scrollClipDisabled(Bool) -> some View` - Sets whether a scroll view clips its content to its bounds. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/scrollclipdisabled(_:)))
- **scrollContentBackground(_:)** `func scrollContentBackground(Visibility) -> some View` - Specifies the visibility of the background for scrollable views within   this view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/scrollcontentbackground(_:)))
- **scrollDisabled(_:)** `func scrollDisabled(Bool) -> some View` - Disables or enables scrolling in scrollable views. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/scrolldisabled(_:)))
- **scrollDismissesKeyboard(_:)** `func scrollDismissesKeyboard(ScrollDismissesKeyboardMode) -> some View` - Configures the behavior in which scrollable content interacts with   the software keyboard. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/scrolldismisseskeyboard(_:)))
- **scrollDismissesKeyboardMode** `var scrollDismissesKeyboardMode: ScrollDismissesKeyboardMode` - The way that scrollable content interacts with the software keyboard. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/scrolldismisseskeyboardmode))
- **scrollEdgeEffectHidden(_:for:)** `func scrollEdgeEffectHidden(Bool, for: Edge.Set) -> some View` - Hides any scroll edge effects for scroll views within this   hierarchy. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/scrolledgeeffecthidden(_:for:)))
- **scrollEdgeEffectStyle(_:for:)** `func scrollEdgeEffectStyle(ScrollEdgeEffectStyle?, for: Edge.Set) -> some View` - Configures the scroll edge effect style for scroll views within this   hierarchy. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/scrolledgeeffectstyle(_:for:)))
- **scrollIndicators(_:axes:)** `func scrollIndicators(ScrollIndicatorVisibility, axes: Axis.Set) -> some View` - Sets the visibility of scroll indicators within this view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/scrollindicators(_:axes:)))
- **scrollIndicatorsFlash(onAppear:)** `func scrollIndicatorsFlash(onAppear: Bool) -> some View` - Flashes the scroll indicators of a scrollable view when it appears. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/scrollindicatorsflash(onappear:)))
- **scrollIndicatorsFlash(trigger:)** `func scrollIndicatorsFlash(trigger: some Equatable) -> some View` - Flashes the scroll indicators of scrollable views when a value changes. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/scrollindicatorsflash(trigger:)))
- **scrollInputBehavior(_:for:)** `func scrollInputBehavior(ScrollInputBehavior, for: ScrollInputKind) -> some View` - Enables or disables scrolling in scrollable views when using particular   inputs. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/scrollinputbehavior(_:for:)))
- **scrollPosition(_:anchor:)** `func scrollPosition(Binding<ScrollPosition>, anchor: UnitPoint?) -> some View` - Associates a binding to a scroll position with a scroll view within this   view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/scrollposition(_:anchor:)))
- **scrollPosition(id:anchor:)** `func scrollPosition(id: Binding<(some Hashable)?>, anchor: UnitPoint?) -> some View` - Associates a binding to be updated when a scroll view within this   view scrolls. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/scrollposition(id:anchor:)))
- **scrollTargetBehavior(_:)** `func scrollTargetBehavior(some ScrollTargetBehavior) -> some View` - Sets the scroll behavior of views scrollable in the provided axes. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/scrolltargetbehavior(_:)))
- **scrollTransition(_:axis:transition:)** `func scrollTransition(ScrollTransitionConfiguration, axis: Axis?, transition: (EmptyVisualEffect, ScrollTransitionPhase) -> some VisualEffect) -> some View` - Applies the given transition, animating between the phases   of the transition as this view appears and disappears within the   visible region of the containing scroll view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/scrolltransition(_:axis:transition:)))
- **scrollTransition(topLeading:bottomTrailing:axis:transition:)** `func scrollTransition(topLeading: ScrollTransitionConfiguration, bottomTrailing: ScrollTransitionConfiguration, axis: Axis?, transition: (EmptyVisualEffect, ScrollTransitionPhase) -> some VisualEffect) -> some View` - Applies the given transition, animating between the phases   of the transition as this view appears and disappears within the   visible region of the containing scroll view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/scrolltransition(topleading:bottomtrailing:axis:transition:)))
- **verticalScrollBounceBehavior** `var verticalScrollBounceBehavior: ScrollBounceBehavior` - The scroll bounce mode for the vertical axis of scrollable views. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/verticalscrollbouncebehavior))
- **verticalScrollIndicatorVisibility** `var verticalScrollIndicatorVisibility: ScrollIndicatorVisibility` - The visiblity to apply to scroll indicators of any   vertically scrollable content. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/verticalscrollindicatorvisibility))
- **webViewOnScrollGeometryChange(for:of:action:)** `func webViewOnScrollGeometryChange<T>(for: T.Type, of: (ScrollGeometry) -> T, action: (T, T) -> Void) -> some View` - Adds an action to be performed when a value, created from a scroll geometry, changes. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/webviewonscrollgeometrychange(for:of:action:)))
- **webViewScrollInputBehavior(_:for:)** `func webViewScrollInputBehavior(ScrollInputBehavior, for: ScrollInputKind) -> some View` - Enables or disables scrolling in web views when using particular inputs. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/webviewscrollinputbehavior(_:for:)))
- **webViewScrollPosition(_:)** `func webViewScrollPosition(Binding<ScrollPosition>) -> some View` - Associates a binding to a scroll position with the web view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/webviewscrollposition(_:)))

## Search

- **DismissSearchAction** `struct DismissSearchAction` - An action that can end a search interaction. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/dismisssearchaction))
- **SearchFieldPlacement** `struct SearchFieldPlacement` - The placement of a search field in a view hierarchy. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/searchfieldplacement))
- **SearchScopeActivation** `struct SearchScopeActivation` - The ways that searchable modifiers can show or hide search scopes. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/searchscopeactivation))
- **SearchSuggestionsPlacement** `struct SearchSuggestionsPlacement` - The ways that SwiftUI displays search suggestions. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/searchsuggestionsplacement))
- **SearchUnavailableContent.Actions** `struct Actions` - A view that represents the actions of a static  ContentUnavailableView.search  view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/searchunavailablecontent/actions))
- **SearchUnavailableContent.Description** `struct Description` - A view that represents the description of a static  ContentUnavailableView.search  view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/searchunavailablecontent/description))
- **SearchUnavailableContent.Label** `struct Label` - A view that represents the label of a static placeholder search view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/searchunavailablecontent/label))
- **dismissSearch** `var dismissSearch: DismissSearchAction` - An action that ends the current search interaction. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/dismisssearch))
- **isSearching** `var isSearching: Bool` - A Boolean value that indicates when the user is searching. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/issearching))
- **onTextEntry** `static var onTextEntry: SearchScopeActivation` - An activation where the system shows search scopes   when typing begins in the search field and hides   search scopes after search cancellation. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/searchscopeactivation/ontextentry))
- **search** `static var search: TabRole` - The search role. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/tabrole/search))
- **searchCompletion(_:)** `func searchCompletion(_:)` - Associates a fully formed string with the value of this view when used   as a search suggestion. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/searchcompletion(_:)))
- **searchDictationBehavior(_:)** `func searchDictationBehavior(TextInputDictationBehavior) -> some View` - Configures the dictation behavior for any search fields configured   by the searchable modifier. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/searchdictationbehavior(_:)))
- **searchFocused(_:)** `func searchFocused(FocusState<Bool>.Binding) -> some View` - Modifies this view by binding the focus state of the search field   associated with the nearest searchable modifier to the given   Boolean value. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/searchfocused(_:)))
- **searchFocused(_:equals:)** `func searchFocused<V>(FocusState<V>.Binding, equals: V) -> some View` - Modifies this view by binding the focus state of the search field   associated with the nearest searchable modifier to the given value. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/searchfocused(_:equals:)))
- **searchScopes(_:activation:_:)** `func searchScopes<V, S>(Binding<V>, activation: SearchScopeActivation, () -> S) -> some View` - Configures the search scopes for this view with the specified   activation strategy. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/searchscopes(_:activation:_:)))
- **searchScopes(_:scopes:)** `func searchScopes<V, S>(Binding<V>, scopes: () -> S) -> some View` - Configures the search scopes for this view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/searchscopes(_:scopes:)))
- **searchSelection(_:)** `func searchSelection(Binding<TextSelection?>) -> some View` - Binds the selection of the search field associated with the nearest   searchable modifier to the given   value. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/searchselection(_:)))
- **searchSuggestions(_:)** `func searchSuggestions<S>(() -> S) -> some View` - Configures the search suggestions for this view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/searchsuggestions(_:)))
- **searchSuggestions(_:for:)** `func searchSuggestions(Visibility, for: SearchSuggestionsPlacement.Set) -> some View` - Configures how to display search suggestions within this view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/searchsuggestions(_:for:)))
- **searchable(text:editableTokens:isPresented:placement:prompt:token:)** `func searchable(text:editableTokens:isPresented:placement:prompt:token:)` - Marks this view as searchable, which configures the display of a   search field. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/searchable(text:editabletokens:ispresented:placement:prompt:token:)))
- **searchable(text:editableTokens:placement:prompt:token:)** `func searchable(text:editableTokens:placement:prompt:token:)` - Marks this view as searchable, which configures the display of a   search field. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/searchable(text:editabletokens:placement:prompt:token:)))
- **searchable(text:isPresented:placement:prompt:)** `func searchable(text:isPresented:placement:prompt:)` - Marks this view as searchable with programmatic presentation of the   search field. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/searchable(text:ispresented:placement:prompt:)))
- **searchable(text:placement:prompt:)** `func searchable(text:placement:prompt:)` - Marks this view as searchable, which configures the display of a   search field. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/searchable(text:placement:prompt:)))
- **searchable(text:placement:prompt:)** `func searchable(text: Binding<String>, placement: SearchFieldPlacement, prompt: Text?) -> some View` - Marks this view as searchable, which configures the display of a   search field. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/searchable(text:placement:prompt:)-18a8f))
- **searchable(text:placement:prompt:suggestions:)** `func searchable(text:placement:prompt:suggestions:)` - Marks this view as searchable, which configures the display of a   search field. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/searchable(text:placement:prompt:suggestions:)))
- **searchable(text:tokens:isPresented:placement:prompt:token:)** `func searchable(text:tokens:isPresented:placement:prompt:token:)` - Marks this view as searchable with text and tokens, as well as   programmatic presentation. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/searchable(text:tokens:ispresented:placement:prompt:token:)))
- **searchable(text:tokens:placement:prompt:token:)** `func searchable(text:tokens:placement:prompt:token:)` - Marks this view as searchable with text and tokens. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/searchable(text:tokens:placement:prompt:token:)))
- **searchable(text:tokens:suggestedTokens:isPresented:placement:prompt:token:)** `func searchable(text:tokens:suggestedTokens:isPresented:placement:prompt:token:)` - Marks this view as searchable with text, tokens, and suggestions, as   well as programmatic presentation. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/searchable(text:tokens:suggestedtokens:ispresented:placement:prompt:token:)))
- **searchable(text:tokens:suggestedTokens:placement:prompt:token:)** `func searchable(text:tokens:suggestedTokens:placement:prompt:token:)` - Marks this view as searchable with text, tokens, and suggestions. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/searchable(text:tokens:suggestedtokens:placement:prompt:token:)))
- **sidebar** `static var sidebar: SearchFieldPlacement` - The search field appears in the sidebar of a navigation view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/searchfieldplacement/sidebar))
- **tabViewSearchActivation(_:)** `func tabViewSearchActivation(TabSearchActivation) -> some View` - Configures the activation and deactivation behavior of search in the   search tab. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/tabviewsearchactivation(_:)))

## Shapes

- **AnyShape** `struct AnyShape` - A type-erased shape value. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/anyshape))
- **AnyShapeStyle** `struct AnyShapeStyle` - A type-erased ShapeStyle value. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/anyshapestyle))
- **ButtonBorderShape** `struct ButtonBorderShape` - A shape used to draw a button’s border. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/buttonbordershape))
- **ContainerRelativeShape** `struct ContainerRelativeShape` - A shape that is replaced by an inset version of the current   container shape. If no container shape was defined, is replaced by   a rectangle. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/containerrelativeshape))
- **ContentShapeKinds** `struct ContentShapeKinds` - A kind for the content shape of a view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/contentshapekinds))
- **DefaultGlassEffectShape** `struct DefaultGlassEffectShape` - The default shape applied by glass effects, a capsule. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/defaultglasseffectshape))
- **FillShapeView** `struct FillShapeView` - A shape provider that fills its shape. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/fillshapeview))
- **OffsetShape** `struct OffsetShape` - A shape with a translation offset transform applied to it. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/offsetshape))
- **RotatedShape** `struct RotatedShape` - A shape with a rotation transform applied to it. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/rotatedshape))
- **RoundedRectangularShape** `protocol RoundedRectangularShape` - A protocol of   that describes a rounded rectangular   shape. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/roundedrectangularshape))
- **RoundedRectangularShapeCorners** `struct RoundedRectangularShapeCorners` - A type describing the corner styles of a  . ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/roundedrectangularshapecorners))
- **ScaledShape** `struct ScaledShape` - A shape with a scale transform applied to it. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scaledshape))
- **Shape** `protocol Shape` - A 2D shape that you can use when drawing a view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/shape))
- **ShapeRole** `enum ShapeRole` - Ways of styling a shape. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/shaperole))
- **ShapeStyle** `protocol ShapeStyle` - A color or pattern to use when rendering a shape. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/shapestyle))
- **ShapeView** `protocol ShapeView` - A view that provides a shape that you can use for drawing operations. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/shapeview))
- **StrokeBorderShapeView** `struct StrokeBorderShapeView` - A shape provider that strokes the border of its shape. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/strokebordershapeview))
- **StrokeShapeView** `struct StrokeShapeView` - A shape provider that strokes its shape. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/strokeshapeview))
- **TransformedShape** `struct TransformedShape` - A shape with an affine transform applied to it. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/transformedshape))
- **blue** `static var blue: Color` - A context-dependent blue color suitable for use in UI elements. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/shapestyle/blue))
- **buttonBorderShape(_:)** `func buttonBorderShape(ButtonBorderShape) -> some View` - Sets the border shape for buttons in this view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/buttonbordershape(_:)))
- **clipShape(_:style:)** `func clipShape<S>(S, style: FillStyle) -> some View` - Sets a clipping shape for this view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/clipshape(_:style:)))
- **containerShape(_:)** `func containerShape(_:)` - Sets the container shape to use for any container relative shape or   concentric rectangle within this view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/containershape(_:)))
- **contentShape(_:_:eoFill:)** `func contentShape<S>(ContentShapeKinds, S, eoFill: Bool) -> some View` - Sets the content shape for this view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/contentshape(_:_:eofill:)))
- **contentShape(_:eoFill:)** `func contentShape<S>(S, eoFill: Bool) -> some View` - Defines the content shape for hit testing. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/contentshape(_:eofill:)))
- **dragPreview** `static let dragPreview: ContentShapeKinds` - The kind for drag and drop previews. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/contentshapekinds/dragpreview))

## SwiftUI

- **ActionSheet** `struct ActionSheet` - A representation of an action sheet presentation. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/actionsheet))
- **Alert** `struct Alert` - A representation of an alert presentation. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/alert))
- **Alignment** `struct Alignment` - An alignment in both axes. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/alignment))
- **Alignment3D** `struct Alignment3D` - An alignment in all three axes. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/alignment3d))
- **AlignmentID** `protocol AlignmentID` - A type that you use to create custom alignment guides. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/alignmentid))
- **AlternatingRowBackgroundBehavior** `struct AlternatingRowBackgroundBehavior` - The styling of views with respect to alternating row backgrounds. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/alternatingrowbackgroundbehavior))
- **Anchor** `struct Anchor` - An opaque value derived from an anchor source and a particular view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/anchor))
- **Angle** `struct Angle` - A geometric angle whose value you access in either radians or degrees. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/angle))
- **AngularGradient** `struct AngularGradient` - An angular gradient. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/angulargradient))
- **AnyCompositorContent** `struct AnyCompositorContent` - Type erased compositor content. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/anycompositorcontent))
- **AnyGradient** `struct AnyGradient` - A color gradient. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/anygradient))
- **AnyTabContent** `struct AnyTabContent` - Type erased tab content. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/anytabcontent))
- **AnyTransition** `struct AnyTransition` - A type-erased transition. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/anytransition))
- **AnyView** `struct AnyView` - A type-erased view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/anyview))
- **AssistiveAccess** `struct AssistiveAccess` - A scene that presents an interface appropriate for Assistive Access on iOS   and iPadOS. On other platforms, this scene is unused. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/assistiveaccess))
- **AsymmetricTransition** `struct AsymmetricTransition` - A composite  Transition  that uses a different transition for   insertion versus removal. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/asymmetrictransition))
- **AutomaticGlassBackgroundEffect** `struct AutomaticGlassBackgroundEffect` - The automatic glass background effect. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/automaticglassbackgroundeffect))
- **Axis** `enum Axis` - The horizontal or vertical dimension in a 2D coordinate system. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/axis))
- **BackgroundProminence** `struct BackgroundProminence` - The prominence of backgrounds underneath other views. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/backgroundprominence))
- **BackgroundTask** `struct BackgroundTask` - The kinds of background tasks that your app or extension can handle. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/backgroundtask))
- **BadgeProminence** `struct BadgeProminence` - The visual prominence of a badge. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/badgeprominence))
- **Bindable** `struct Bindable` - A property wrapper type that supports creating bindings to the mutable   properties of observable objects. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/bindable))
- **Binding** `struct Binding` - A property wrapper type that can read and write a value owned by a source of   truth. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/binding))
- **BlendMode** `enum BlendMode` - Modes for compositing a view with overlapping content. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/blendmode))
- **Body** `associatedtype Body : View` - The type of view representing the body of this view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/body-swift.associatedtype))
- **BreakthroughEffect** `struct BreakthroughEffect` ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/breakthrougheffect))
- **Canvas** `struct Canvas` - A view type that supports immediate mode drawing. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/canvas))
- **Capsule** `struct Capsule` - A capsule shape aligned inside the frame of the view containing it. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/capsule))
- **Chirality** `enum Chirality` - The chirality, or handedness, of a pose. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/chirality))
- **Circle** `struct Circle` - A circle centered on the frame of the view containing it. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/circle))
- **Color** `struct Color` - A representation of a color that adapts to a given context. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/color))
- **ColorMatrix** `struct ColorMatrix` - A matrix to use in an RGBA color transformation. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/colormatrix))
- **ColorRenderingMode** `enum ColorRenderingMode` - The set of possible working color spaces for color-compositing operations. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/colorrenderingmode))
- **ColorScheme** `enum ColorScheme` - The possible color schemes, corresponding to the light and dark appearances. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/colorscheme))
- **ColorSchemeContrast** `enum ColorSchemeContrast` - The contrast between the app’s foreground and background colors. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/colorschemecontrast))
- **CommandGroup** `struct CommandGroup` - Groups of controls that you can add to existing command menus. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/commandgroup))
- **CommandGroupPlacement** `struct CommandGroupPlacement` - The standard locations that you can place new command groups relative to. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/commandgroupplacement))
- **CommandMenu** `struct CommandMenu` - Command menus are stand-alone, top-level containers for controls that   perform related, app-specific commands. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/commandmenu))
- **Commands** `protocol Commands` - Conforming types represent a group of related commands that can be exposed   to the user via the main menu on macOS and key commands on iOS. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/commands))
- **CommandsBuilder** `struct CommandsBuilder` - Constructs command sets from multi-expression closures. Like  ViewBuilder ,   it supports up to ten expressions in the closure body. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/commandsbuilder))
- **CompositorContent** `protocol CompositorContent` ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/compositorcontent))
- **CompositorContentBuilder** `struct CompositorContentBuilder` - A result builder for composing a collection of    elements. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/compositorcontentbuilder))
- **ConcentricRectangle** `struct ConcentricRectangle` - A shape that is replaced by a concentric version of the current container   shape. If the container shape is a rectangle derived shape with four   corners, this shape could choose to respect corners individually. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/concentricrectangle))
- **ContainerBackgroundPlacement** `struct ContainerBackgroundPlacement` - The placement of a container background. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/containerbackgroundplacement))
- **ContainerValueKey** `protocol ContainerValueKey` - A key for accessing container values. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/containervaluekey))
- **ContainerValues** `struct ContainerValues` - A collection of container values associated with a given view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/containervalues))
- **ContentHoverEffect** `struct ContentHoverEffect` - A  CustomHoverEffect  that applies effects to a view on hover using a   closure. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/contenthovereffect))
- **ContentMarginPlacement** `struct ContentMarginPlacement` - The placement of margins. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/contentmarginplacement))
- **ContentMode** `enum ContentMode` - Constants that define how a view’s content fills the available space. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/contentmode))
- **ContentMode.fill** `case fill` - An option that resizes the content so it occupies all available space,   both vertically and horizontally. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/contentmode/fill))
- **ContentMode.fit** `case fit` - An option that resizes the content so it’s all within the available space,   both vertically and horizontally. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/contentmode/fit))
- **ContentSizeCategory** `enum ContentSizeCategory` - The sizes that you can specify for content. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/contentsizecategory))
- **ContentTransition** `struct ContentTransition` - A kind of transition that applies to the content within a single view,   rather than to the insertion or removal of a view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/contenttransition))
- **ContentUnavailableView** `struct ContentUnavailableView` - An interface, consisting of a label and additional content, that you   display when the content of your app is unavailable to users. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/contentunavailableview))
- **CoordinateSpace** `enum CoordinateSpace` - A resolved coordinate space created by the coordinate space protocol. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/coordinatespace))

- **Transition** `protocol Transition` - A description of view changes to apply when a view is added to and removed   from the view hierarchy. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/transition))
- **TransitionPhase** `enum TransitionPhase` - An indication of which the current stage of a transition. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/transitionphase))
- **TransitionProperties** `struct TransitionProperties` - The properties a  Transition  can have. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/transitionproperties))
- **TupleView** `struct TupleView` - A View created from a swift tuple of View values. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/tupleview))
- **TypesettingLanguage** `struct TypesettingLanguage` - Defines how typesetting language is determined for text. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/typesettinglanguage))
- **UIHostingConfiguration** `struct UIHostingConfiguration` - A content configuration suitable for hosting a hierarchy of SwiftUI views. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/uihostingconfiguration))
- **UIHostingOrnament** `class UIHostingOrnament` - A model that represents an ornament suitable for being hosted in UIKit. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/uihostingornament))
- **UIOrnament** `class UIOrnament` - The abstract base class that represents an ornament. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/uiornament))
- **UnevenRoundedRectangle** `struct UnevenRoundedRectangle` - A rectangular shape with rounded corners with different values, aligned   inside the frame of the view containing it. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/unevenroundedrectangle))
- **UnitCurve** `struct UnitCurve` - A  function defined by a two-dimensional curve that maps an input   progress in the range [0,1] to an output progress that is also in the   range [0,1]. By changing the shape of the curve, the effective speed   of an animation or other interpolation can be changed. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/unitcurve))
- **UnitPoint** `struct UnitPoint` - A normalized 2D point in a view’s coordinate space. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/unitpoint))
- **UnitPoint3D** `struct UnitPoint3D` - A normalized 3D point in a view’s coordinate space. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/unitpoint3d))
- **UserInterfaceSizeClass** `enum UserInterfaceSizeClass` - A set of values that indicate the visual size available to the view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/userinterfacesizeclass))
- **VSplitView** `struct VSplitView` - A layout container that arranges its children in a vertical line and allows   the user to resize them using dividers placed between them. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/vsplitview))
- **VStack** `struct VStack` - A view that arranges its subviews in a vertical line. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/vstack))
- **VectorArithmetic** `protocol VectorArithmetic` - A type that can serve as the animatable data of an animatable type. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/vectorarithmetic))
- **VerticalAlignment** `struct VerticalAlignment` - An alignment position along the vertical axis. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/verticalalignment))
- **VerticalEdge** `enum VerticalEdge` - An edge on the vertical axis. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/verticaledge))
- **View** `protocol View` - A type that represents part of your app’s user interface and provides   modifiers that you use to configure views. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view))
- **ViewBuilder** `struct ViewBuilder` - A custom parameter attribute that constructs views from closures. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/viewbuilder))
- **ViewDimensions** `struct ViewDimensions` - A view’s size and alignment guides in its own coordinate space. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/viewdimensions))
- **ViewDimensions3D** `struct ViewDimensions3D` - A view’s 3D size and alignment guides in its own coordinate space. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/viewdimensions3d))
- **ViewModifier** `protocol ViewModifier` - A modifier that you apply to a view or another view modifier, producing a   different version of the original value. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/viewmodifier))
- **ViewSpacing** `struct ViewSpacing` - A collection of the geometric spacing preferences of a view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/viewspacing))
- **ViewThatFits** `struct ViewThatFits` - A view that adapts to the available space by providing the first   child view that fits. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/viewthatfits))
- **Viewpoint3D** `struct Viewpoint3D` - A type describing what direction something is being viewed from. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/viewpoint3d))
- **Visibility** `enum Visibility` - The visibility of a UI element, chosen automatically based on   the platform, current context, and other factors. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/visibility))
- **VisualEffect** `protocol VisualEffect` - Visual Effects change the visual appearance of a view without changing its   ancestors or descendents. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/visualeffect))
- **VolumeViewpointUpdateStrategy** `struct VolumeViewpointUpdateStrategy` - A type describing when the action provided to    should be   called. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/volumeviewpointupdatestrategy))
- **WKExtensionDelegateAdaptor** `struct WKExtensionDelegateAdaptor` - A property wrapper type that you use to create a WatchKit extension delegate. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/wkextensiondelegateadaptor))
- **Widget** `protocol Widget` - The configuration and content of a widget to display on the Home screen or   in Notification Center. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/widget))
- **WidgetBundle** `protocol WidgetBundle` - A container used to expose multiple widgets from a single widget extension. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/widgetbundle))
- **WidgetConfiguration** `protocol WidgetConfiguration` - A type that describes a widget’s content. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/widgetconfiguration))
- **WorldAlignmentBehavior** `struct WorldAlignmentBehavior` - A type representing the world alignment behavior for a scene. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/worldalignmentbehavior))
- **WorldRecenterPhase** `enum WorldRecenterPhase` - A type that represents information associated with a phase of a   system recenter event. Values of this type are passed to the closure   specified in View.onWorldRecenter(action:). ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/worldrecenterphase))
- **WorldScalingBehavior** `struct WorldScalingBehavior` - Specifies the scaling behavior a window should have within the world. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/worldscalingbehavior))
- **WorldScalingCompensation** `struct WorldScalingCompensation` - Indicates whether returned metrics will take dynamic scaling into account. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/worldscalingcompensation))
- **WorldTrackingLimitation** `struct WorldTrackingLimitation` - A structure to represent limitations of tracking the user’s surroundings. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/worldtrackinglimitation))
- **WritingToolsBehavior** `struct WritingToolsBehavior` - The Writing Tools editing experience for text and text input. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/writingtoolsbehavior))
- **ZStack** `struct ZStack` - A view that overlays its subviews, aligning them in both axes. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/zstack))
- **ZStackContent3D** `struct ZStackContent3D` - A type that adds spacing to a  . ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/zstackcontent3d))

Both  automatic  and  visible  will show the baseplate.   hidden  will never show it. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/volumebaseplatevisibility(_:)))

([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/withtransaction(_:_:_:)))

([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/writingtoolsaffordancevisibility(_:)))

## Tables

- **AXChartDescriptorRepresentable** `protocol AXChartDescriptorRepresentable` - A type to generate an  AXChartDescriptor  object that you use to provide   information about a chart and its data for an accessible experience   in VoiceOver or other assistive technologies. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/axchartdescriptorrepresentable))
- **AdaptableTabBarPlacement** `struct AdaptableTabBarPlacement` - A placement for tabs in a tab view using the adaptable sidebar style. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/adaptabletabbarplacement))
- **Animatable** `protocol Animatable` - A type that describes how to animate a property of a view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/animatable))
- **Animatable()** `macro Animatable()` - A member and extension macro that, when applied to a struct, class or enum   declaration, synthesizes the conformance to  Animatable  and its   requirement, the  animatableData  property using the existing animatable   properties of the type this macro is applied to. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/animatable()))
- **AnimatableModifier** `protocol AnimatableModifier` - A modifier that can create another modifier with animation. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/animatablemodifier))
- **AnimatablePair** `struct AnimatablePair` - A pair of animatable values, which is itself animatable. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/animatablepair))
- **AnimatableValues** `struct AnimatableValues` ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/animatablevalues))
- **DisclosureTableRow** `struct DisclosureTableRow` - A kind of table row that shows or hides additional rows based on the state   of a disclosure control. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/disclosuretablerow))
- **DynamicTableRowContent** `protocol DynamicTableRowContent` - A type of table row content that generates table rows from an underlying   collection of data. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/dynamictablerowcontent))
- **EditableCollectionContent** `struct EditableCollectionContent` - An opaque wrapper view that adds editing capabilities to a row in a list. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/editablecollectioncontent))
- **EmptyAnimatableData** `struct EmptyAnimatableData` - An empty type for animatable data. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/emptyanimatabledata))
- **EmptyTableRowContent** `struct EmptyTableRowContent` - A table row content that doesn’t produce any rows. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/emptytablerowcontent))
- **EquatableView** `struct EquatableView` - A view type that compares itself against its previous value and prevents its   child updating if its new value is the same as its old value. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/equatableview))
- **InsettableShape** `protocol InsettableShape` - A shape type that is able to inset itself to produce another shape. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/insettableshape))
- **NSViewControllerRepresentable** `protocol NSViewControllerRepresentable` - A wrapper that you use to integrate an AppKit view controller into your   SwiftUI interface. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/nsviewcontrollerrepresentable))
- **NSViewControllerRepresentableContext** `struct NSViewControllerRepresentableContext` - Contextual information about the state of the system that you use to create   and update your AppKit view controller. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/nsviewcontrollerrepresentablecontext))
- **NSViewRepresentable** `protocol NSViewRepresentable` - A wrapper that you use to integrate an AppKit view into your SwiftUI view   hierarchy. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/nsviewrepresentable))
- **NSViewRepresentableContext** `struct NSViewRepresentableContext` - Contextual information about the state of the system that you use to create   and update your AppKit view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/nsviewrepresentablecontext))
- **Table** `struct Table` - A container that presents rows of data arranged in one or more columns,   optionally providing the ability to select one or more members. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/table))
- **TableColumn** `struct TableColumn` - A column that displays a view for each row in a table. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/tablecolumn))
- **TableColumnAlignment** `struct TableColumnAlignment` - Describes the alignment of the content of a table column. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/tablecolumnalignment))
- **TableColumnBuilder** `struct TableColumnBuilder` - A result builder that creates table column content from closures. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/tablecolumnbuilder))
- **TableColumnContent** `protocol TableColumnContent` - A type used to represent columns within a table. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/tablecolumncontent))
- **TableColumnCustomization** `struct TableColumnCustomization` - A representation of the state of the columns in a table. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/tablecolumncustomization))
- **TableColumnCustomizationBehavior** `struct TableColumnCustomizationBehavior` - A set of customization behaviors of a column that a table can offer to   a user. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/tablecolumncustomizationbehavior))
- **TableColumnForEach** `struct TableColumnForEach` - A structure that computes columns on demand from an underlying collection of   identified data. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/tablecolumnforeach))
- **TableForEachContent** `struct TableForEachContent` - A type of table row content that creates table rows created by iterating   over a collection. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/tableforeachcontent))
- **TableHeaderRowContent** `struct TableHeaderRowContent` - A table row that displays a single view instead of columned content. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/tableheaderrowcontent))
- **TableOutlineGroupContent** `struct TableOutlineGroupContent` - An opaque table row type created by a table’s hierarchical initializers. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/tableoutlinegroupcontent))
- **TableRow** `struct TableRow` - A row that represents a data value in a table. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/tablerow))
- **TableRowBuilder** `struct TableRowBuilder` - A result builder that creates table row content from closures. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/tablerowbuilder))
- **TableRowContent** `protocol TableRowContent` - A type used to represent table rows. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/tablerowcontent))
- **TableStyle** `protocol TableStyle` - A type that applies a custom appearance to all tables within a view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/tablestyle))
- **TableStyleConfiguration** `struct TableStyleConfiguration` - The properties of a table. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/tablestyleconfiguration))
- **TupleTableRowContent** `struct TupleTableRowContent` - A type of table column content that creates table rows created from a   Swift tuple of table rows. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/tupletablerowcontent))
- **UIViewControllerRepresentable** `protocol UIViewControllerRepresentable` - A view that represents a UIKit view controller. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/uiviewcontrollerrepresentable))
- **UIViewControllerRepresentableContext** `struct UIViewControllerRepresentableContext` - Contextual information about the state of the system that you use to create   and update your UIKit view controller. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/uiviewcontrollerrepresentablecontext))
- **UIViewRepresentable** `protocol UIViewRepresentable` - A wrapper for a UIKit view that you use to integrate that view into your   SwiftUI view hierarchy. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/uiviewrepresentable))
- **UIViewRepresentableContext** `struct UIViewRepresentableContext` - Contextual information about the state of the system that you use to create   and update your UIKit view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/uiviewrepresentablecontext))
- **WKInterfaceObjectRepresentable** `protocol WKInterfaceObjectRepresentable` - A view that represents a WatchKit interface object. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/wkinterfaceobjectrepresentable))
- **WKInterfaceObjectRepresentableContext** `struct WKInterfaceObjectRepresentableContext` - Contextual information about the state of the system that you use to create   and update your WatchKit interface object. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/wkinterfaceobjectrepresentablecontext))
- **cuttable(for:action:)** `func cuttable<T>(for: T.Type, action: () -> [T]) -> some View` - Specifies an action that moves items to the Clipboard in response to   the system’s Cut command. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/cuttable(for:action:)))
- **defaultAdaptableTabBarPlacement(_:)** `func defaultAdaptableTabBarPlacement(AdaptableTabBarPlacement) -> some View` - Specifies the default placement for the tabs in a tab view using the   adaptable sidebar style. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/defaultadaptabletabbarplacement(_:)))

- **tableStyle(_:)** `func tableStyle<S>(S) -> some View` - Sets the style for tables within this view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/tablestyle(_:)))

## Text input and output

- **AttributedTextFormatting** `enum AttributedTextFormatting` - A namespace for types related to attributed text formatting definitions. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/attributedtextformatting))
- **AttributedTextFormattingDefinition** `protocol AttributedTextFormattingDefinition` - A protocol for defining how text can be styled in a view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/attributedtextformattingdefinition))
- **AttributedTextSelection** `struct AttributedTextSelection` - Represents a selection of attributed text. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/attributedtextselection))
- **AttributedTextValueConstraint** `protocol AttributedTextValueConstraint` - A protocol for defining a constraint on the value of a certain attribute. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/attributedtextvalueconstraint))
- **ContextMenu** `struct ContextMenu` - A container for views that you present as menu items in a context menu. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/contextmenu))
- **FindContext** `struct FindContext` - The status of the find navigator for views which support text editing. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/findcontext))
- **FixedTextVariant** `struct FixedTextVariant` - The default text variant preference that chooses the largest available   variant. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/fixedtextvariant))
- **ImmersionChangeContext** `struct ImmersionChangeContext` - A structure that represents a state of immersion of your app. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/immersionchangecontext))
- **PreviewContext** `protocol PreviewContext` - A context type for use with a preview. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/previewcontext))
- **PreviewContextKey** `protocol PreviewContextKey` - A key type for a preview context. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/previewcontextkey))
- **SizeDependentTextVariant** `struct SizeDependentTextVariant` - The size dependent variant preference allows the text to take the available   space into account when choosing the variant to display. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/sizedependenttextvariant))
- **Text** `struct Text` - A view that displays one or more lines of read-only text. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/text))
- **TextAlignment** `enum TextAlignment` - An alignment position for text along the horizontal axis. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/textalignment))
- **TextAttribute** `protocol TextAttribute` - A value that you can attach to text views and that text renderers can query. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/textattribute))
- **TextEditingCommands** `struct TextEditingCommands` - A built-in group of commands for searching, editing, and transforming   selections of text. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/texteditingcommands))
- **TextEditor** `struct TextEditor` - A view that can display and edit long-form text. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/texteditor))
- **TextEditorStyle** `protocol TextEditorStyle` - A specification for the appearance and interaction of a text editor. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/texteditorstyle))
- **TextEditorStyleConfiguration** `struct TextEditorStyleConfiguration` - The properties of a text editor. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/texteditorstyleconfiguration))
- **TextField** `struct TextField` - A control that displays an editable text interface. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/textfield))
- **TextFieldLink** `struct TextFieldLink` - A control that requests text input from the user when pressed. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/textfieldlink))
- **TextFieldStyle** `protocol TextFieldStyle` - A specification for the appearance and interaction of a text field. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/textfieldstyle))
- **TextFormattingCommands** `struct TextFormattingCommands` - A built-in set of commands for transforming the styles applied to selections   of text. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/textformattingcommands))
- **TextInputAutocapitalization** `struct TextInputAutocapitalization` - The kind of autocapitalization behavior applied during text input. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/textinputautocapitalization))
- **TextInputDictationActivation** `struct TextInputDictationActivation` ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/textinputdictationactivation))
- **TextInputDictationBehavior** `struct TextInputDictationBehavior` ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/textinputdictationbehavior))
- **TextProxy** `struct TextProxy` - A proxy for a text view that custom text renderers use. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/textproxy))
- **TextRenderer** `protocol TextRenderer` - A value that can replace the default text view rendering behavior. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/textrenderer))
- **TextSelectability** `protocol TextSelectability` - A type that describes the ability to select text. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/textselectability))
- **TextSelection** `struct TextSelection` - Represents a selection of text. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/textselection))
- **TextSelectionAffinity** `enum TextSelectionAffinity` - A representation of the direction or association of a selection or cursor   relative to a text character. This concept becomes much more prominent   when dealing with bidirectional text (text that contains both LTR and RTL   scripts, like English and Arabic combined). ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/textselectionaffinity))
- **TextVariantPreference** `protocol TextVariantPreference` - A protocol for controlling the size variant of text views. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/textvariantpreference))
- **TimelineViewDefaultContext** `typealias TimelineViewDefaultContext` - Information passed to a timeline view’s content callback. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/timelineviewdefaultcontext))
- **WindowPlacementContext** `struct WindowPlacementContext` - A type which represents contextual information used for sizing and   positioning windows. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/windowplacementcontext))
- **attributedTextFormattingDefinition(_:)** `func attributedTextFormattingDefinition(_:)` - Apply a text formatting definition to nested views. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/attributedtextformattingdefinition(_:)))
- **bold()** `func bold() -> Text` - Applies a bold or emphasized treatment to the fonts of the text. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/text/bold()))
- **contextMenu(_:)** `func contextMenu<MenuItems>(ContextMenu<MenuItems>?) -> some View` - Adds a context menu to the view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/contextmenu(_:)))
- **contextMenu(forSelectionType:menu:primaryAction:)** `func contextMenu<I, M>(forSelectionType: I.Type, menu: (Set<I>) -> M, primaryAction: ((Set<I>) -> Void)?) -> some View` - Adds an item-based context menu to a view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/contextmenu(forselectiontype:menu:primaryaction:)))
- **contextMenu(menuItems:)** `func contextMenu<MenuItems>(menuItems: () -> MenuItems) -> some View` - Adds a context menu to a view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/contextmenu(menuitems:)))
- **contextMenu(menuItems:preview:)** `func contextMenu<M, P>(menuItems: () -> M, preview: () -> P) -> some View` - Adds a context menu with a custom preview to a view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/contextmenu(menuitems:preview:)))
- **documentBrowserContextMenu(_:)** `func documentBrowserContextMenu(([URL]?) -> some View) -> some View` - Adds to a  DocumentLaunchView  actions that accept   a list of selected files as their parameter. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/documentbrowsercontextmenu(_:)))
- **firstTextBaseline** `static let firstTextBaseline: VerticalAlignment` - A guide that marks the top-most text baseline in a view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/verticalalignment/firsttextbaseline))
- **font(_:)** `func font(Font?) -> Text` - Sets the default font for text in the view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/text/font(_:)))
- **managedObjectContext** `var managedObjectContext: NSManagedObjectContext` ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/managedobjectcontext))
- **modelContext(_:)** `func modelContext(ModelContext) -> some View` - Sets the model context in this view’s environment. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/modelcontext(_:)))
- **multilineTextAlignment** `var multilineTextAlignment: TextAlignment` - An environment value that indicates how a text view aligns its lines   when the content wraps or contains newlines. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/multilinetextalignment))
- **multilineTextAlignment(_:)** `func multilineTextAlignment(TextAlignment) -> some View` - Sets the alignment of a text view that contains multiple lines of text. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/multilinetextalignment(_:)))
- **multilineTextAlignment(strategy:)** `func multilineTextAlignment(strategy: Text.AlignmentStrategy) -> some View` - A modifier for the default text alignment strategy in the view   hierarchy. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/multilinetextalignment(strategy:)))
- **previewContext(_:)** `func previewContext<C>(C) -> some View` - Declares a context for the preview. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/previewcontext(_:)))
- **textCase** `var textCase: Text.Case?` - A stylistic override to transform the case of  Text  when displayed,   using the environment’s locale. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/textcase))
- **textCase(_:)** `func textCase(Text.Case?) -> some View` - Sets a transform for the case of the text contained in this view when   displayed. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/textcase(_:)))
- **textContentType(_:)** `func textContentType(_:)` - Sets the text content type for this view, which the system uses to   offer suggestions while the user enters text on macOS. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/textcontenttype(_:)))
- **textContentType(_:)** `func textContentType(WKTextContentType?) -> some View` - Sets the text content type for this view, which the system uses to offer   suggestions while the user enters text on a watchOS device. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/textcontenttype(_:)-4dqqb))
- **textContentType(_:)** `func textContentType(NSTextContentType?) -> some View` - Sets the text content type for this view, which the system uses to   offer suggestions while the user enters text on macOS. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/textcontenttype(_:)-6fic1))
- **textContentType(_:)** `func textContentType(UITextContentType?) -> some View` - Sets the text content type for this view, which the system uses to   offer suggestions while the user enters text on an iOS or tvOS device. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/textcontenttype(_:)-ufdv))
- **textEditorStyle(_:)** `func textEditorStyle(some TextEditorStyle) -> some View` - Sets the style for text editors within this view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/texteditorstyle(_:)))
- **textFieldStyle(_:)** `func textFieldStyle<S>(S) -> some View` - Sets the style for text fields within this view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/textfieldstyle(_:)))
- **textInputAutocapitalization(_:)** `func textInputAutocapitalization(TextInputAutocapitalization?) -> some View` - Sets how often the shift key in the keyboard is automatically enabled. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/textinputautocapitalization(_:)))
- **textInputCompletion(_:)** `func textInputCompletion(String) -> some View` - Associates a fully formed string with the value of this view when used   as a text input suggestion ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/textinputcompletion(_:)))
- **textInputSuggestions(_:)** `func textInputSuggestions<S>(() -> S) -> some View` - Configures the text input suggestions for this view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/textinputsuggestions(_:)))
- **textInputSuggestions(_:content:)** `func textInputSuggestions<Data, Content>(Data, content: (Data.Element) -> Content) -> some View` - Configures the text input suggestions for this view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/textinputsuggestions(_:content:)))
- **textInputSuggestions(_:id:content:)** `func textInputSuggestions<Data, ID, Content>(Data, id: KeyPath<Data.Element, ID>, content: (Data.Element) -> Content) -> some View` - Configures the text input suggestions for this view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/textinputsuggestions(_:id:content:)))
- **textRenderer(_:)** `func textRenderer<T>(T) -> some View` - Returns a new view such that any text views within it will use   renderer  to draw themselves. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/textrenderer(_:)))
- **textScale(_:isEnabled:)** `func textScale(Text.Scale, isEnabled: Bool) -> some View` - Applies a text scale to text in the view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/textscale(_:isenabled:)))
- **textSelection(_:)** `func textSelection<S>(S) -> some View` - Controls whether people can select text within this view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/textselection(_:)))
- **textSelectionAffinity** `var textSelectionAffinity: TextSelectionAffinity` - A representation of the direction or association of a selection or cursor   relative to a text character. This concept becomes much more prominent   when dealing with bidirectional text (text that contains both LTR and RTL   scripts, like English and Arabic combined). ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/textselectionaffinity))
- **textSelectionAffinity(_:)** `func textSelectionAffinity(TextSelectionAffinity) -> some View` - Sets the direction of a selection or cursor relative to a text character. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/textselectionaffinity(_:)))
- **webViewContextMenu(menu:)** `func webViewContextMenu(menu: (WebView.ActivatedElementInfo) -> some View) -> some View` - Adds an item-based context menu to a WebView, replacing the default set of context menu items.
