## Toolbars

- **ContentToolbarPlacement** `struct ContentToolbarPlacement` ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/contenttoolbarplacement))
- **CustomizableToolbarContent** `protocol CustomizableToolbarContent` - Conforming types represent items that can be placed in various locations   in a customizable toolbar. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/customizabletoolbarcontent))
- **DefaultToolbarItem** `struct DefaultToolbarItem` - A toolbar item that represents a system component. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/defaulttoolbaritem))
- **LabeledToolbarItemGroupContent** `struct LabeledToolbarItemGroupContent` - A view that represents the view of a toolbar item group with a specified   label. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/labeledtoolbaritemgroupcontent))
- **SearchToolbarBehavior** `struct SearchToolbarBehavior` - The behavior of a search field in a toolbar. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/searchtoolbarbehavior))
- **ToolbarCommands** `struct ToolbarCommands` - A built-in set of commands for manipulating window toolbars. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/toolbarcommands))
- **ToolbarContent** `protocol ToolbarContent` - Conforming types represent items that can be placed in various locations   in a toolbar. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/toolbarcontent))
- **ToolbarContentBuilder** `struct ToolbarContentBuilder` - Constructs a toolbar item set from multi-expression closures. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/toolbarcontentbuilder))
- **ToolbarCustomizationBehavior** `struct ToolbarCustomizationBehavior` - The customization behavior of customizable toolbar content. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/toolbarcustomizationbehavior))
- **ToolbarCustomizationOptions** `struct ToolbarCustomizationOptions` - Options that influence the default customization behavior of   customizable toolbar content. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/toolbarcustomizationoptions))
- **ToolbarDefaultItemKind** `struct ToolbarDefaultItemKind` - A kind of toolbar item a  View  adds by default. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/toolbardefaultitemkind))
- **ToolbarItem** `struct ToolbarItem` - A model that represents an item which can be placed in the toolbar   or navigation bar. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/toolbaritem))
- **ToolbarItemGroup** `struct ToolbarItemGroup` - A model that represents a group of  ToolbarItem s which can be placed in   the toolbar or navigation bar. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/toolbaritemgroup))
- **ToolbarItemPlacement** `struct ToolbarItemPlacement` - A structure that defines the placement of a toolbar item. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/toolbaritemplacement))
- **ToolbarLabelStyle** `struct ToolbarLabelStyle` - The label style of a toolbar. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/toolbarlabelstyle))
- **ToolbarPlacement** `struct ToolbarPlacement` - The placement of a toolbar. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/toolbarplacement))
- **ToolbarRole** `struct ToolbarRole` - The purpose of content that populates the toolbar. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/toolbarrole))
- **ToolbarSpacer** `struct ToolbarSpacer` - A standard space item in toolbars. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/toolbarspacer))
- **ToolbarTitleDisplayMode** `struct ToolbarTitleDisplayMode` - A type that defines the behavior of title of a toolbar. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/toolbartitledisplaymode))
- **ToolbarTitleMenu** `struct ToolbarTitleMenu` - The title menu of a toolbar. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/toolbartitlemenu))
- **WindowToolbarFullScreenVisibility** `struct WindowToolbarFullScreenVisibility` - The visibility of the window toolbar with respect to full screen mode. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/windowtoolbarfullscreenvisibility))
- **WindowToolbarStyle** `protocol WindowToolbarStyle` - A specification for the appearance and behavior of a window’s toolbar. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/windowtoolbarstyle))
- **contentToolbar(for:content:)** `func contentToolbar(for:content:)` - Populates the toolbar of the specified content view type with   the views you provide. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/contenttoolbar(for:content:)))
- **inline** `static var inline: ToolbarTitleDisplayMode` - The inline mode. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/toolbartitledisplaymode/inline))
- **largeTitle** `static let largeTitle: ToolbarItemPlacement` - Places the item in the title area of the navigation bar. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/toolbaritemplacement/largetitle))
- **presentedWindowToolbarStyle(_:)** `func presentedWindowToolbarStyle<S>(S) -> some View` - Sets the style for the toolbar in windows created by   interacting with this view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/presentedwindowtoolbarstyle(_:)))
- **searchToolbarBehavior(_:)** `func searchToolbarBehavior(SearchToolbarBehavior) -> some View` - Configures the behavior for search in the toolbar. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/searchtoolbarbehavior(_:)))
- **title** `static var title: ToolbarItemPlacement` - Places the item in the title area of the navigation bar. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/toolbaritemplacement/title))
- **toolbar(_:for:)** `func toolbar(Visibility, for: ToolbarPlacement...) -> some View` - Specifies the visibility of a bar managed by SwiftUI. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/toolbar(_:for:)))
- **toolbar(content:)** `func toolbar(content:)` - Populates the toolbar or navigation bar with the specified items. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/toolbar(content:)))
- **toolbar(id:content:)** `func toolbar<Content>(id: String, content: () -> Content) -> some View` - Populates the toolbar or navigation bar with the specified items,   allowing for user customization. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/toolbar(id:content:)))
- **toolbar(removing:)** `func toolbar(removing: ToolbarDefaultItemKind?) -> some View` - Remove a toolbar item present by default ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/toolbar(removing:)))
- **toolbarBackground(_:for:)** `func toolbarBackground(_:for:)` - Specifies the preferred shape style of the background of a bar managed   by SwiftUI. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/toolbarbackground(_:for:)))
- **toolbarBackgroundVisibility(_:for:)** `func toolbarBackgroundVisibility(Visibility, for: ToolbarPlacement...) -> some View` - Specifies the preferred visibility of backgrounds on a bar managed by   SwiftUI. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/toolbarbackgroundvisibility(_:for:)))
- **toolbarColorScheme(_:for:)** `func toolbarColorScheme(ColorScheme?, for: ToolbarPlacement...) -> some View` - Specifies the preferred color scheme of a bar managed by SwiftUI. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/toolbarcolorscheme(_:for:)))
- **toolbarForegroundStyle(_:for:)** `func toolbarForegroundStyle<S>(S, for: ToolbarPlacement...) -> some View` - Specifies the preferred foreground style of bars managed by SwiftUI. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/toolbarforegroundstyle(_:for:)))
- **toolbarItemHidden(_:)** `func toolbarItemHidden(Bool) -> some View` - Hides an individual view within a control group toolbar item. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/toolbaritemhidden(_:)))
- **toolbarLabelStyle** `var toolbarLabelStyle: ToolbarLabelStyle?` - The label style to apply to controls within a toolbar. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/environmentvalues/toolbarlabelstyle))
- **toolbarRole(_:)** `func toolbarRole(ToolbarRole) -> some View` - Configures the semantic role for the content populating the toolbar. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/toolbarrole(_:)))
- **toolbarTitleDisplayMode(_:)** `func toolbarTitleDisplayMode(ToolbarTitleDisplayMode) -> some View` - Configures the toolbar title display mode for this view. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/toolbartitledisplaymode(_:)))
- **toolbarTitleMenu(content:)** `func toolbarTitleMenu<C>(content: () -> C) -> some View` - Configure the title menu of a toolbar. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/toolbartitlemenu(content:)))
- **toolbarVisibility(_:for:)** `func toolbarVisibility(Visibility, for: ToolbarPlacement...) -> some View` - Specifies the visibility of a bar managed by SwiftUI. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/toolbarvisibility(_:for:)))
- **windowToolbarFullScreenVisibility(_:)** `func windowToolbarFullScreenVisibility(WindowToolbarFullScreenVisibility) -> some View` - Configures the visibility of the window toolbar when the window enters   full screen mode. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/view/windowtoolbarfullscreenvisibility(_:)))
- **windowToolbarLabelStyle(_:)** `func windowToolbarLabelStyle(Binding<ToolbarLabelStyle>) -> some Scene` - Sets the label style of items in a toolbar and enables user customization. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scene/windowtoolbarlabelstyle(_:)))
- **windowToolbarLabelStyle(fixed:)** `func windowToolbarLabelStyle(fixed: ToolbarLabelStyle) -> some Scene` - Sets the label style of items in a toolbar. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scene/windowtoolbarlabelstyle(fixed:)))
- **windowToolbarStyle(_:)** `func windowToolbarStyle<S>(S) -> some Scene` - Sets the style for the toolbar defined within this scene. ([docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/swiftui/scene/windowtoolbarstyle(_:)))

## Showcase Strategy

The app demonstrates common, portable, renderable SwiftUI APIs directly. The generated full index is bundled as `swiftui-api-index.json` and merged into the app catalog as reference entries, so APIs that cannot be instantiated in a generic cross-platform demo still appear one by one in the browser.
